&2^{(1+\beta)j_{2}}\|g_{2}\|_{L^{2}}+2^{(1-\beta)j_{2}}\|h_{2}\|_{L^{2}}+2^{\gamma j_{2}}\sup_{R\in[2^{-j_{2}},2^{k_{2}}],\theta_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}R^{-2}\|\widehat{h_{2}}\|_{L^{1}(B(\theta_{0},R))}\lesssim 1.\end{split}$ (6.58) Then, we define the functions $g_{1}^{v},h_{1}^{v},g_{2}^{v},h_{2}^{v}$ by the formulas (compare with ( 6.49 )), $\begin{split}&\widehat{g_{1}^{v}}(\theta):=\varphi(2^{-50D}\kappa^{-1}(\theta-\kappa v+p^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\kappa v)))\cdot\mathcal{F}(P_{[k_{1}-2,k_{1}+2]}g_{1})(\theta),\\
&\widehat{h_{1}^{v}}(\theta):=\varphi(2^{-50D}\kappa^{-1}(\theta-\kappa v+p^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\kappa v)))\cdot\mathcal{F}(P_{[k_{1}-2,k_{1}+2]}h_{1})(\theta),\\
&\widehat{g_{2}^{v}}(\theta):=\varphi(2^{-50D}\kappa^{-1}(\theta-p^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\kappa v)))\cdot\mathcal{F}(P_{[k_{2}-2,k_{2}+2]}g_{2})(\theta),\\
&\widehat{h_{2}^{v}}(\theta):=\varphi(2^{-50D}\kappa^{-1}(\theta-p^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\kappa v)))\cdot\mathcal{F}(P_{[k_{2}-2,k_{2}+2]}h_{2})(\theta).\end{split}$ (6.59) As in ( 6.50 ), using $L^{2}$ orthogonality and ( 6.58 ), we have $\begin{split}&\sum_{v\in Z_{\kappa}^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}}\|g_{1}^{v}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim 2^{-2j_{1}-2\beta j_{1}},\quad\sum_{v\in Z_{\kappa}^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}}\|h_{1}^{v}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim 2^{-2j_{1}+2\beta j_{1}},\\
&\sum_{v\in Z_{\kappa}^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}}\|g_{2}^{v}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim 2^{-2j_{2}-2\beta j_{2}},\quad\sum_{v\in Z_{\kappa}^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}}\|h_{2}^{v}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim 2^{-2j_{2}+2\beta j_{2}}.\end{split}$ (6.60) Let $E^{\mu}_{s}f=e^{-is\widetilde{\Lambda}_{\mu}}f$ . Using ( 6.15 ), and either ( A.37 ), ( A.42 ), ( A.49 ) or ( A.55 ) together with ( 6.57 )–( 6.58 ), we derive the $L^{\infty}$ bounds $\begin{split}&\|E_{s}^{\mu}g_{1}^{v}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{-3m/2}\|g_{1}^{v}\|_{L^{1}}\lesssim 2^{-3m/2}2^{(1/2-\beta)j_{1}},\\
&\|E_{s}^{\mu}h_{1}^{v}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|\widehat{h^{v}_{1}}\|_{L^{1}}\lesssim\kappa^{2}2^{-\gamma j_{1}},\\
&\|E_{s}^{\nu}g_{2}^{v}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{-3m/2}\|g_{2}^{v}\|_{L^{1}}\lesssim 2^{-3m/2}2^{(1/2-\beta)j_{2}},\\
&\|E_{s}^{\nu}h_{2}^{v}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|\widehat{h^{v}_{2}}\|_{L^{1}}\lesssim\kappa^{2}2^{-\gamma j_{2}},\end{split}$ (6.61) for any $v\in Z_{\kappa}^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}$ . Using ( 6.53 ) and ( 6.60 )–( 6.61 ), we estimate, assuming $j_{1}\leq j_{2}$ , $\begin{split}2^{4m+2\beta m}\kappa&\sum_{v\in Z_{\kappa}^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}}\big[\|A_{v}(E_{s}^{\mu}g_{1}^{v},E_{s}^{\nu}g_{2}^{v})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|A_{v}(E_{s}^{\mu}h^{v}_{1},E_{s}^{\nu}g_{2}^{v})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\big]\\
&\lesssim 2^{4m+2\beta m}\kappa\sum_{v\in Z_{\kappa}^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}}\|g_{2}^{v}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}(\|E_{s}^{\nu}g_{1}^{v}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\|E_{s}^{\nu}h_{1}^{v}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2})\\
&\lesssim 2^{4m+2\beta m}\kappa\cdot 2^{-2j_{2}-2\beta j_{2}}\cdot[2^{-3m}2^{(1-2\beta)j_{1}}+\kappa^{4}2^{-2\gamma j_{1}}]\\
&\lesssim 2^{3m}2^{(2\beta+\beta^{2})m}2^{-(1+2\beta)j_{2}}\cdot 2^{-3m}2^{(1-2\beta)j_{2}}\\
&\lesssim 2^{-\beta^{3}m}.\end{split}$ Similarly, we estimate $\begin{split}2^{4m+2\beta m}\kappa&\sum_{v\in Z_{\kappa}^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}}\big[\|A_{v}(E_{s}^{\mu}g_{1}^{v},E_{s}^{\nu}h_{2}^{v})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|A_{v}(E_{s}^{\mu}h^{v}_{1},E_{s}^{\nu}h_{2}^{v})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\big]\\
&\lesssim 2^{4m+2\beta m}\kappa\sum_{v\in Z_{\kappa}^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}}\|E_{s}^{\nu}h_{2}^{v}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}(\|E_{s}^{\nu}g_{1}^{v}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|E_{s}^{\nu}h_{1}^{v}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})\\
&\lesssim 2^{4m+2\beta m}\kappa\cdot\kappa^{4}2^{-2\gamma j_{2}}\cdot 2^{-2j_{1}+2\beta j_{1}}\\
&\lesssim 2^{-m/10}.\end{split}$ The desired estimate ( 6.52 ) follows from the last two bounds and the restriction ( 6.56 ). This completes the proof of the lemma.
∎ 7. Proof of Proposition 4.3 , III: Case B resonant interactions In this section we consider type B interactions, see Proposition B.2 , and prove the following proposition: Proposition 7.1 . Assume that $(k,j),(k_{1},j_{1}),(k_{2},j_{2})\in\mathcal{J}$ , $m\in[1,L]\cap\mathbb{Z}$ , $\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}\in\in\mathcal{T}_{B}=\{\Phi^{e;i+,e+},\Phi^{e;i-,e+},\Phi^{b;i+,b+},\Phi^{b;i-,b+}\},$ (7.1) and $\begin{split}&-9m/10\leq k_{1},k_{2}\leq j/N_{0}^{\prime},\quad\max(j_{1},j_{2})\leq(1-\beta/10)m,\quad\beta m/2+N_{0}^{\prime}k_{+}+D^{2}\leq j\leq m+D,\\
&k_{1}\leq-D/3,\qquad k\geq-D/4,\qquad|k-k_{2}|\leq 10.\end{split}$ (7.2) Then there is $\kappa\in(0,1]$ , $\kappa\geq\max\big(2^{(\beta^{2}m-m)/2}2^{-k_{1}/2},2^{\beta^{2}m-m}2^{\max(j_{1},j_{2})}\big)$ , such that $(1+2^{k})\big\|\widetilde{\varphi}^{(k)}_{j}\cdot P_{k}R_{m,\kappa}^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(f_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu},f_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu})\big\|_{B^{1}_{k,j}}\lesssim 2^{-2\beta^{4}m}.$ (7.3) The rest of the section is concerned with the proof of Proposition 7.1 . We have assumed, without loss of generality, that $k_{1}\leq k_{2}$ . As in Case A, the proof of the proposition relies on a careful analysis of resonant interactions. For this analysis we need to understand well the geometry of almost resonant sets. For $\sigma\in\{e,b\}$ let $R_{\sigma}$ denote the unique solutions in $(0,\infty)$ of the equations $\lambda^{\prime}_{\sigma}(R_{\sigma})=\lambda^{\prime}_{i}(0)=\sqrt{(1+T)/(1+\varepsilon)}.$ (7.4) The numbers $R_{\sigma}$ are well-defined, in view of Lemma A.4 , and $R_{\sigma}\approx_{\varepsilon,C_{b}}1$ . For $(\mu,\nu)\in\{(i+,e+),(i-,e+),(i+,b+),(i-,b+)\}$ , $\mu=(i\iota_{1})$ , $\nu=(\sigma_{2}+)$ , $\sigma_{2}\in\{e,b\}$ , we define the functions $r^{\mu,\nu}:(R_{\sigma_{2}}-2^{-D/5},R_{\sigma_{2}}+2^{-D/5})\to(R_{\sigma_{2}}-2^{-D/10},R_{\sigma_{2}}+2^{-D/10})$ as the unique solutions of the equations $\lambda^{\prime}_{\sigma_{2}}(r^{\mu,\nu}(s))-\lambda^{\prime}_{i}(s-r^{\mu,\nu}(s))=0.$ (7.5) Notice that these functions are well defined for $s\in(R_{\sigma_{2}}-2^{-D/5},R_{\sigma_{2}}+2^{-D/5})$ , since the functions $r\to\lambda^{\prime}_{\sigma_{2}}(r)-\lambda^{\prime}_{i}(s-r)$ are strictly increasing and vanish in the appropriate ranges, as a consequence of Lemma A.4 (i) and the observation that $\lambda^{\prime\prime}_{i}(0)=0$ . Moreover, $|(\partial_{s}r^{\mu,\nu})(s)|\approx_{C_{b},\varepsilon}|s-r^{\mu,\nu}(s)|\qquad\text{ for any }s\in(R_{\sigma_{2}}-2^{-D/5},R_{\sigma_{2}}+2^{-D/5}).$ (7.6) Lemma 7.2 . Assume that $\mu=(i\iota_{1})$ , $\iota_{1}\in\{+,-\}$ , $\nu=(\sigma_{2}+)$ , $\sigma_{2}\in\{e,b\}$ , $k,k_{1},k_{2}\in\mathbb{Z}$ , $k_{1}\leq-D/3$ , $k\geq-D/4$ , $|k-k_{2}|\leq 10$ , and $\delta\in[0,2^{-10D}]$ . Assume that there is a point $(\xi,\eta)\in\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}$ satisfying $|\xi|\in[2^{k-4},2^{k+4}],\quad|\eta|\in[2^{k_{2}-4},2^{k_{2}+4}],\quad|\xi-\eta|\in[2^{k_{1}-4},2^{k_{1}+4}],\quad|\Xi^{\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)|\leq\delta.$ (7.7) (i) Then $k,k_{2}\in[-D/100,D/100],\quad\text{ and }\quad\big||\xi|-R_{\sigma_{2}}\big|+\big||\eta|-R_{\sigma_{2}}\big|\lesssim_{C_{b},\varepsilon}2^{k_{1}}+\delta.$ (7.8) More precisely, if $\xi=se$ for some $s>0$ and some unit vector $e\in\mathbb{S}^{2}$ then $\begin{split}&|s-R_{\sigma_{2}}|\lesssim_{C_{b},\varepsilon}2^{k_{1}}+\delta,\\
&\eta=re+\eta^{\prime},\qquad|r-r^{\mu,\nu}(s)|\lesssim_{C_{b},\varepsilon}\delta,\qquad|r-s|\approx_{C_{b},\varepsilon}2^{k_{1}},\qquad\iota_{1}|s-r|=s-r,\\
&\eta^{\prime}\cdot e=0,\qquad|\eta^{\prime}|\lesssim_{C_{b},\varepsilon}2^{k_{1}}\delta.\end{split}$ (7.9) (ii) If, in addition, $\delta\leq 2^{k_{1}-D/10}$ then $\begin{split}&\text{ if }\quad\iota_{1}=+\quad\text{ then }\quad s-R_{\sigma_{2}}\approx_{C_{b},\varepsilon}2^{k_{1}}\text{ and }R_{\sigma_{2}}-r^{\mu,\nu}(s)\approx_{C_{b},\varepsilon}2^{2k_{1}},\\
&\text{ if }\quad\iota_{1}=-\quad\text{ then }\quad R_{\sigma_{2}}-s\approx_{C_{b},\varepsilon}2^{k_{1}}\text{ and }R_{\sigma_{2}}-r^{\mu,\nu}(s)\approx_{C_{b},\varepsilon}2^{2k_{1}}.\end{split}$ (7.10) Proof of Lemma 7.2 . (i) We start from the formula $\Xi^{\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)=-\iota_{1}\lambda^{\prime}_{i}(|\eta-\xi|)\frac{\eta-\xi}{|\eta-\xi|}-\lambda^{\prime}_{\sigma_{2}}(|\eta|)\frac{\eta}{|\eta|}.$ (7.11) Since $\big|\lambda^{\prime}_{i}(|\eta-\xi|)-\lambda^{\prime}_{i}(0)\big|\lesssim_{\varepsilon,C_{b}}2^{2k_{1}}$ , the condition $|\Xi^{\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)|\leq\delta$ and the estimates in Lemma A.4 (i) show that $\big||\eta|-R_{\sigma_{2}}\big|\lesssim_{\varepsilon,C_{b}}2^{2k_{1}}+\delta$ . The desired bounds in ( 7.8 ) follow. We prove now the claims in ( 7.9 ). Letting $\xi=se$ for some $s>0,e\in\mathbb{S}^{2}$ and $\eta=re+\eta^{\prime}$ , $r\in\mathbb{R}$ , $\eta^{\prime}\cdot e=0$ , the condition $|\Xi^{\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)|\leq\delta$ and the formula ( 7.11 ) show that $\begin{split}&\Big|-\iota_{1}\lambda^{\prime}_{i}(\sqrt{(r-s)^{2}+|\eta^{\prime}|^{2}})\frac{r-s}{\sqrt{(r-s)^{2}+|\eta^{\prime}|^{2}}}-\lambda^{\prime}_{\sigma_{2}}(\sqrt{r^{2}+|\eta^{\prime}|^{2}})\frac{r}{\sqrt{r^{2}+|\eta^{\prime}|^{2}}}\Big|\leq\delta,\\
&\Big|-\iota_{1}\lambda^{\prime}_{i}(\sqrt{(r-s)^{2}+|\eta^{\prime}|^{2}})\frac{\eta^{\prime}}{\sqrt{(r-s)^{2}+|\eta^{\prime}|^{2}}}-\lambda^{\prime}_{\sigma_{2}}(\sqrt{r^{2}+|\eta^{\prime}|^{2}})\frac{\eta^{\prime}}{\sqrt{r^{2}+|\eta^{\prime}|^{2}}}\Big|\leq\delta.\end{split}$ (7.12) Recall that $\lambda^{\prime}_{i}(0)>0$ . Recalling also the assumptions ( 7.7 ) and the bounds ( 7.8 ), the second equation in ( 7.12 ) shows that $|\eta^{\prime}|\lesssim_{C_{b},\varepsilon}2^{k_{1}}\delta$ as desired. In addition, $|s-r|\approx_{C_{b},\varepsilon}2^{k_{1}}$ , therefore $\big|s-R_{\sigma_{2}}\big|+\big|r-R_{\sigma_{2}}\big|\lesssim_{C_{b},\varepsilon}2^{k_{1}}+\delta.$ The first equation in ( 7.12 ) now gives $\Big|\iota_{1}\lambda^{\prime}_{i}(|r-s|)\frac{r-s}{|r-s|}+\lambda^{\prime}_{\sigma_{2}}(r)\Big|\leq 2\delta.$ (7.13) Since $\lambda^{\prime}_{\sigma_{2}}(r)\approx_{C_{b},\varepsilon}1$ it follows that $\iota_{1}(r-s)=-|r-s|$ and, therefore, $\Big|-\lambda^{\prime}_{i}(s-r)+\lambda^{\prime}_{\sigma_{2}}(r)\Big|\leq 2\delta.$ Finally, we notice that the derivative of the map $r\to-\lambda^{\prime}_{i}(s-r)+\lambda^{\prime}_{\sigma_{2}}(r)$ is $\approx_{C_{b},\varepsilon}1$ is the appropriate ranges of $r,s$ , therefore $|r-r^{\mu,\nu}(s)|\lesssim_{C_{b},\varepsilon}\delta$ . This completes the proof of ( 7.12 ). (ii) If $\delta\leq 2^{k_{1}-D/10}$ then, using ( 7.9 ), $|s-r^{\mu,\nu}(s)|\approx_{C_{b},\varepsilon}2^{k_{1}}$ . Therefore, using Lemma A.4 (i), $\lambda^{\prime}_{i}(0)-\lambda^{\prime}_{i}(s-r^{\mu,\nu}(s))\approx_{C_{b},\varepsilon}2^{2k_{1}}$ . Using the definition ( 7.5 ) it follows that $R_{\sigma_{2}}-r^{\mu,\nu}(s)\approx_{C_{b},\varepsilon}2^{2k_{1}}$ . Therefore $|r-R_{\sigma_{2}}|\lesssim_{C_{b},\varepsilon}2^{2k_{1}}+\delta$ . The remaining bounds in ( 7.10 ) now follow from the identity $\iota_{1}|s-r|=s-r$ (see ( 7.9 )) and the assumption $\delta+2^{2k_{1}}\leq 2^{k_{1}-D/10}$ .
∎ 7.1. Proof of Proposition 7.1 We further divide the proof into several lemmas. Lemma 7.3 . The bound ( 7.3 ) holds if ( 7.2 ) holds and, in addition, $\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}\in\{\Phi^{e;e+,i+},\Phi^{e;e+,i-},\Phi^{b;b+,i+},\Phi^{b;b+,i-}\}\text{ or }k\notin[-D/100,D/100]\text{ or }k_{2}\notin[-D/100,D/100],$ (7.14) with $\kappa:=2^{-10D}.$ (7.15) Proof of Lemma 7.3 . In any of these cases we have $P_{k}R_{m,\kappa}^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(f_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu},f_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu})=0$ , using either Lemma A.4 (i) (which shows that $\lambda^{\prime}_{i}(r)\approx_{C_{b},\varepsilon}1$ , $r\in[0,\infty)$ , and $\lambda^{\prime}_{e}(r)\approx\lambda^{\prime}_{b}(r)\approx_{C_{b},\varepsilon}r$ , $r\in[0,1]$ ) or Lemma 7.2 .
∎ Lemma 7.4 . The bound ( 7.3 ) holds if ( 7.2 ) holds and, in addition, $\begin{split}&\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}\in\{\Phi^{e;i+,e+},\Phi^{e;i-,e+},\Phi^{b;i+,b+},\Phi^{b;i-,b+}\},\\
&k,k_{2}\in[-D/100,D/100],\qquad\max(j_{1},j_{2})\leq(m-\beta^{2}m)/2-k_{1}/2,\end{split}$ (7.16) with $\kappa:=2^{(\beta^{2}m-m)/2-k_{1}/2}.$ (7.17) Proof of Lemma 7.4 . Let $\begin{split}G(\xi):&=\varphi_{k}(\xi)\cdot\mathcal{F}\big[R_{m,\kappa}^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(f_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu},f_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu})\big](\xi)\\
&=\varphi_{k}(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}\chi_{R}^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)q_{m}(s)\cdot\widehat{f_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}}(\xi-\eta,s)\widehat{f_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}}(\eta,s)\,d\eta ds.\end{split}$ (7.18) where, as before, $\chi_{R}^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)=\varphi(2^{D^{2}+\max(0,k_{1},k_{2})}\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta))\varphi(|\Xi^{\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)|/\kappa).$ We have to prove that $2^{(1+\beta)j}\|\widetilde{\varphi}^{(k)}_{j}\cdot\mathcal{F}^{-1}(G)\|_{L^{2}}+\|G\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{-2\beta^{4}m}.$ (7.19) Using Lemma 7.2 and the $L^{\infty}$ bounds in ( 5.17 ), for any $\xi\in\mathbb{R}^{3}$ we have $\begin{split}|G(\xi)|&\lesssim 2^{m}\cdot 2^{2k_{1}}\kappa^{2}\min(2^{k_{1}},\kappa)\cdot 2^{-k_{1}/2}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{[-2^{D}(2^{k_{1}}+\kappa),2^{D}(2^{k_{1}}+\kappa)]}(|\xi|-R_{\sigma_{2}})\\
&\lesssim 2^{2\beta^{2}m}2^{k_{1}/2}\min(2^{k_{1}},\kappa)\cdot\mathbf{1}_{[-2^{D}(2^{k_{1}}+\kappa),2^{D}(2^{k_{1}}+\kappa)]}(|\xi|-R_{\sigma_{2}}).\end{split}$ (7.20) The $L^{\infty}$ bound in ( 7.19 ) follows. To prove the $L^{2}$ bound in ( 7.19 ) we notice first that we may assume that $2^{j}\lesssim 2^{\beta^{2}m}2^{m}(\kappa+2^{k_{1}})$ , which is stronger than the assumption $j\leq m+D$ in ( 7.2 ). Indeed, assuming that $\xi=se$ , $\eta=re+\eta^{\prime}$ satisfy ( 7.7 ) with $\delta=2\kappa$ , we estimate $\big|(\nabla_{\xi}\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu})(\xi,\eta)\big|=\Big|-(\nabla_{\eta}\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu})(\xi,\eta)+[\nabla\Lambda_{\sigma}(\xi)-\nabla\Lambda_{\sigma}(\eta)]\Big|\lesssim 2^{k_{1}}+\kappa.$ (7.21) Therefore, we make the change of variables $\eta=\xi-\theta$ in ( 7.18 ) and integrate by parts in $\xi$ using ( 5.17 ) and Lemma A.2 with $K\approx 2^{\beta^{2}m}2^{m}(\kappa+2^{k_{1}})$ , $\epsilon^{-1}\approx 2^{m}(\kappa+2^{k_{1}})$ . It follows that $2^{(1+\beta)j}\|\widetilde{\varphi}^{(k)}_{j}\cdot\mathcal{F}^{-1}(G)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-m}\qquad\text{ if }\qquad 2^{j}\geq 2^{\beta^{2}m}2^{m}(\kappa+2^{k_{1}}).$ Therefore, for ( 7.19 ) it suffices to prove that $2^{(1+\beta)m}(\kappa+2^{k_{1}})^{1+\beta}\|G\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-2\beta^{2}m}.$ (7.22) Case 1. It follows from ( 7.20 ) that the left-hand side of ( 7.22 ) is dominated by $C2^{(1+\beta)m}2^{2\beta^{2}m}2^{k_{1}/2}2^{k_{1}}\kappa(2^{k_{1}}+\kappa)^{1/2}\lesssim 2^{(1/2+2\beta)m}(2^{3k_{1}/2}+2^{k_{1}}\kappa^{1/2}).$ The desired bound ( 7.22 ) follows if $k_{1}\leq-m/3-4\beta m$ . Case 2. Assume now that $-m/3+\beta m\leq k_{1}\leq-D/3.$ (7.23) In this case we need to improve on the bound ( 7.20 ).
We use Lemma 7.2 with $\delta=2\kappa$ , and notice that, as a consequence of ( 7.23 ), $\delta\leq 2^{k_{1}-D/10}$ . Assuming $\xi=se$ and $\eta=re+\eta^{\prime}$ satisfy ( 7.7 ) we estimate, using also Lemma A.4 , Lemma 7.2 , and ( 7.13 ), $\begin{split}\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)&=\lambda_{\sigma_{2}}(s)-\iota_{1}\lambda_{i}(\sqrt{(r-s)^{2}+|\eta^{\prime}|^{2}})-\lambda_{\sigma_{2}}(\sqrt{r^{2}+|\eta^{\prime}|^{2}})\\
&=\lambda_{\sigma_{2}}(s)-\iota_{1}\lambda_{i}(|r-s|)-\lambda_{\sigma_{2}}(r)+O_{C_{b},\varepsilon}(\kappa^{2})\\
&=\lambda_{\sigma_{2}}(s)-\lambda_{\sigma_{2}}(r)-\lambda_{i}(s-r)+O_{C_{b},\varepsilon}(\kappa^{2})\\
&=\lambda_{\sigma_{2}}(s)-\lambda_{\sigma_{2}}(R_{\sigma_{2}})-\lambda_{i}(s-R_{\sigma_{2}})+O_{C_{b},\varepsilon}(\kappa^{2}+2^{3k_{1}})\\
&=\lambda_{\sigma_{2}}(s)-\lambda_{\sigma_{2}}(R_{\sigma_{2}})-\lambda^{\prime}_{\sigma_{2}}(R_{\sigma_{2}})\cdot(s-R_{\sigma_{2}})+O_{C_{b},\varepsilon}(\kappa^{2}+2^{3k_{1}})\\
&\approx_{C_{b},\varepsilon}2^{2k_{1}}.\end{split}$ (7.24) We can integrate by parts in $s$ in the formula ( 7.18 ) to conclude that $\begin{split}|G(\xi)|\lesssim 2^{-2k_{1}}|\varphi_{k}(\xi)|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}&|\varphi(|\Xi^{\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)|/\kappa)|\,|q^{\prime}_{m}(s)|\,|\widehat{f_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}}(\xi-\eta,s)|\,|\widehat{f_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}}(\eta,s)|\\
&+|\varphi(|\Xi^{\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)|/\kappa)|\,|q_{m}(s)|\,|(\partial_{s}\widehat{f_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}})(\xi-\eta,s)|\,|\widehat{f_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}}(\eta,s)|\\
&+|\varphi(|\Xi^{\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)|/\kappa)|\,|q_{m}(s)|\,|\widehat{f_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}}(\xi-\eta,s)|\,|(\partial_{s}\widehat{f_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}})(\eta,s)|\,d\eta ds.\end{split}$ (7.25) We use now ( 5.7 ), the last bound in ( 5.17 ), and the bound ( 5.22 ). In view of Lemma 7.2 , the volume of integration is $\approx(2^{k_{1}}\kappa)^{2}\kappa$ and it follows that $\begin{split}|G(\xi)|&\lesssim 2^{-2k_{1}}\mathbf{1}_{[0,2^{D}]}(2^{-k_{1}}|s-R_{\sigma_{2}}|)\cdot 2^{2k_{1}}\kappa^{3}\cdot 2^{\beta m/10}2^{-k_{1}}\\
&\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{[0,2^{D}]}(2^{-k_{1}}|s-R_{\sigma_{2}}|)\cdot 2^{-3m/2}2^{\beta m/5}2^{-5k_{1}/2}.\end{split}$ (7.26) Therefore the left-hand side of ( 7.22 ) is dominated by $2^{(1+\beta)m}2^{k_{1}}\cdot 2^{-3m/2}2^{\beta m/5}2^{-2k_{1}}\lesssim 2^{-k_{1}}2^{-m/2}2^{2\beta m},$ and the desired bound ( 7.22 ) follows using also ( 7.23 ). Case 3. It remains to prove the bound ( 7.22 ) in the case $-m/3-4\beta m\leq k_{1}\leq-m/3+\beta m\quad\text{ and }\quad 2^{-m/3-\beta m}\leq\kappa\leq 2^{-m/3+3\beta m}.$ (7.27) We define $G^{\prime}(\xi):=\varphi_{k}(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}\varphi(2^{D^{2}+\max(0,k_{1},k_{2})}\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta))q_{m}(s)\cdot\widehat{f_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}}(\xi-\eta,s)\widehat{f_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}}(\eta,s)\,d\eta ds$ and notice that, using integration by parts in $\eta$ as in the proof of Lemma 5.8 , $\|G-G^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-10m}.$ Moreover, using Lemma A.3 and the $L^{\infty}$ bounds ( 7.57 ) below, $\begin{split}\|G^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}&\lesssim 2^{m}\sup_{s\in[2^{m-1},2^{m+1}]}\min\left\{\|Ef^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}\|f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}(s)\|_{L^{2}},\|f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\|Ef^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}\right\}\\
&\lesssim 2^{m}\cdot 2^{-3m/2}2^{-\max(j_{1},j_{2})(1/2-2\beta)}.\end{split}$ The desired bound ( 7.22 ) follows if $\max(j_{1},j_{2})\geq m/2$ , using also ( 7.27 ). Finally, assume that $-m/3-4\beta m\leq k_{1}\leq-m/3+\beta m,\qquad 2^{-m/3-\beta m}\leq\kappa\leq 2^{-m/3+3\beta m},\qquad\max(j_{1},j_{2})\leq m/2.$ (7.28) In this case we need to improve slightly on the pointwise bound ( 7.20 ). Assuming $\xi=r^{\prime}e$ , $r^{\prime}\in(0,\infty)$ , $e\in\mathbb{S}^{2}$ and letting $\eta=re+\eta^{\prime}$ , $\eta^{\prime}\cdot e=0$ , we define, for any $l\in\mathbb{Z}$ , $\begin{split}G^{\prime}_{\leq l}(\xi):=&\varphi_{k}(r^{\prime}e)\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}e^{is\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(r^{\prime}e,re+\eta^{\prime})}\varphi(2^{D^{2}+\max(0,k_{1},k_{2})}\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(r^{\prime}e,re+\eta^{\prime}))q_{m}(s)\\
&\times\varphi((r-r^{\mu,\nu}(r^{\prime}))/2^{l}\kappa)\varphi(\eta^{\prime}/(2^{D+k_{1}}\kappa))\cdot\widehat{f_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}}(r^{\prime}e-re-\eta^{\prime},s)\widehat{f_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}}(re+\eta^{\prime},s)\,drd\eta^{\prime}ds.\end{split}$ Clearly, $\|G-G^{\prime}_{\leq D}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-10m}$ , see ( 7.9 ). Estimating as in ( 7.20 ), $\begin{split}|G^{\prime}_{\leq l}(\xi)|&\lesssim 2^{m}\cdot 2^{2k_{1}}\kappa^{2}2^{l}\kappa\cdot 2^{-k_{1}/2}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{[-2^{D}(2^{k_{1}}+\kappa),2^{D}(2^{k_{1}}+\kappa)]}(|\xi|-R_{\sigma_{2}})\\
&\lesssim 2^{l}2^{2\beta^{2}m-m/2}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{[-2^{D}(2^{k_{1}}+\kappa),2^{D}(2^{k_{1}}+\kappa)]}(|\xi|-R_{\sigma_{2}}).\end{split}$ Therefore, setting $l_{0}:=-\lfloor 8\beta m\rfloor$ we estimate $2^{(1+\beta)m}(\kappa+2^{k_{1}})^{1+\beta}\|G^{\prime}_{\leq l_{0}}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{m+\beta m}2^{-m/3+3\beta m}\cdot 2^{l_{0}}2^{2\beta^{2}m-m/2}2^{-m/6+3\beta m/2}\lesssim 2^{-\beta^{2}m}.$ (7.29) Finally we notice that we can integrate by parts in $r$ and use Lemma A.2 with $K\approx 2^{m}2^{l}\kappa$ and $\epsilon^{-1}\approx 2^{\beta m}[2^{\max(j_{1},j_{2})}+2^{-l}(2^{k_{1}}+\kappa)^{-1}]$ to show that $|G_{\leq l+1}(\xi)-G_{\leq l}(\xi)|\lesssim 2^{-10m}$ (7.30) if $l\in[l_{0},D]$ . Indeed, it follows from ( 7.28 ) that $K\epsilon\gtrsim 2^{\beta m}$ . Moreover, for $(r,\eta^{\prime})$ in the relevant support, $\begin{split}\Big|\frac{d}{dr}\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(r^{\prime}e,re+\eta^{\prime})\Big|&=\Big|-\iota_{1}\lambda^{\prime}_{i}(\sqrt{(r-r^{\prime})^{2}+|\eta^{\prime}|^{2}})\frac{r-r^{\prime}}{\sqrt{(r-r^{\prime})^{2}+|\eta^{\prime}|^{2}}}-\lambda^{\prime}_{\sigma_{2}}(\sqrt{r^{2}+|\eta^{\prime}|^{2}})\frac{r}{\sqrt{r^{2}+|\eta^{\prime}|^{2}}}\Big|\\
&=\Big|\frac{\iota_{1}(r^{\prime}-r)}{|r-r^{\prime}|}\lambda^{\prime}_{i}(r^{\prime}-r)-\lambda^{\prime}_{\sigma_{2}}(r)\Big|+O_{C_{b},\varepsilon}(\kappa^{2}+2^{2k_{1}})\\
&\gtrsim_{C_{b},\varepsilon}2^{l}\kappa.\end{split}$ The bound ( 7.30 ) follows from Lemma A.2 . The desired estimate ( 7.22 ) follows using also ( 7.29 ).
∎ Lemma 7.5 . The bound ( 7.3 ) holds if ( 7.2 ) holds and, in addition, $\begin{split}&\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}\in\{\Phi^{e;i+,e+},\Phi^{e;i-,e+},\Phi^{b;i+,b+},\Phi^{b;i-,b+}\},\\
&k,k_{2}\in[-D/100,D/100],\qquad\max(j_{1},j_{2})\geq(m-\beta^{2}m)/2-k_{1}/2,\end{split}$ (7.31) with $\kappa:=2^{\max(j_{1},j_{2})+\beta^{2}m-m}.$ (7.32) Proof of Lemma 7.5 . We define the function $G$ as in ( 7.18 ); it suffices to prove that $2^{(1+\beta)j}\|\widetilde{\varphi}^{(k)}_{j}\cdot\mathcal{F}^{-1}(G)\|_{L^{2}}+\|G\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{-2\beta^{4}m}.$ (7.33) The $L^{\infty}$ bound in ( 7.33 ) is easy: if $j_{1}\leq j_{2}$ then we use the bounds $\begin{split}&\|\widehat{f_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{-k_{1}/2},\\
&|\varphi_{k}(\xi)|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big|\widehat{f_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}}(\eta,s)\big|\,\big|\varphi(|\Xi^{\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)|/\kappa)\big|\mathbf{1}_{[2^{k_{1}-4},2^{k_{1}+4}]}(|\xi-\eta|)\,d\eta\lesssim 2^{-(1+\beta)j_{2}}(\kappa^{3}2^{2k_{1}})^{1/2},\end{split}$ which follow from Lemma 7.2 , the bounds ( 5.15 ), and Definition 4.1 . Therefore, in this case, $\|G\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{m}\cdot 2^{-k_{1}/2}2^{-(1+\beta)j_{2}}(\kappa^{3}2^{2k_{1}})^{1/2}\lesssim 2^{-\beta m/4}2^{(j_{2}-m)/8},$ which suffices. Similarly, if $j_{1}\geq j_{2}$ then we use the bounds $\begin{split}&\|\widehat{f_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 1,\\
&|\varphi_{k}(\xi)|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big|\widehat{f_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}}(\xi-\eta,s)\big|\,\big|\varphi(|\Xi^{\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)|/\kappa)\big|\mathbf{1}_{[2^{k_{2}-4},2^{k_{2}+4}]}(|\eta|)\,d\eta\lesssim 2^{-(1+\beta)j_{1}}(\kappa^{3}2^{2k_{1}})^{1/2},\end{split}$ and the desired $L^{\infty}$ bound on $G$ follows as before. The $L^{2}$ bound in ( 7.33 ) is more complicated. We notice first that the same argument as in the proof of Lemma 7.4 , using the estimate ( 7.21 ), shows that $2^{(1+\beta)j}\|\widetilde{\varphi}^{(k)}_{j}\cdot\mathcal{F}^{-1}(G)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-m}\qquad\text{ if }\qquad 2^{j}\geq 2^{\beta^{2}m}2^{m}(\kappa+2^{k_{1}}).$ (7.34) To continue we consider three cases. Case 1. Assume first that $2^{k_{1}-D}\leq\kappa.$ (7.35) In view of ( 7.34 ), in this case it remains to prove that $2^{(1+\beta)m}\kappa^{1+\beta}\|G\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-2\beta^{2}m}.$ (7.36) We argue as in the proof of Lemma 5.7 . We define first $G^{\prime}(\xi):=\varphi_{k}(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}\varphi(2^{D^{2}+\max(0,k_{1},k_{2})}\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta))q_{m}(s)\cdot\widehat{f_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}}(\xi-\eta,s)\widehat{f_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}}(\eta,s)\,d\eta ds$ and notice that, using integration by parts in $\eta$ and Lemma A.2 with $K\approx 2^{m}\kappa$ , $\epsilon^{-1}\approx 2^{\max(j_{1},j_{2})}$ , $\|G-G^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-10m}.$ (7.37) Since $\|\widetilde{\varphi}_{j_{1}}^{(k_{1})}\cdot P_{k_{1}}f_{\mu}(s)\|_{B_{k_{1},j_{1}}}+\|\widetilde{\varphi}_{j_{2}}^{(k_{2})}\cdot P_{k_{2}}f_{\nu}(s)\|_{B_{k_{2},j_{2}}}\lesssim 1$ , see ( 5.15 ), we use ( 4.6 )–( 4.9 ) to decompose $\begin{split}&\widetilde{\varphi}^{(k_{1})}_{j_{1}}\cdot P_{k_{1}}f_{\mu}(s)=2^{-\alpha k_{1}}[g_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s)+h_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s)],\\
&2^{(1+{\beta})j_{1}}\|g_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s)\|_{L^{2}}+2^{(1/2-\beta)\widetilde{k_{1}}}\|\widehat{g_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 1,\\
&2^{(1-{\beta})j_{1}}\|h_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s)\|_{L^{2}}+\|\widehat{h_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}+2^{\gamma j_{1}}\|\widehat{h_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}}(s)\|_{L^{1}}\lesssim 2^{-8|k_{1}|},\end{split}$ (7.38) and $\begin{split}&\widetilde{\varphi}^{(k_{2})}_{j_{2}}\cdot P_{k_{2}}f_{\nu}(s)=[g_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s)+h_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s)],\\
&2^{(1+{\beta})j_{2}}\|g_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s)\|_{L^{2}}+\|\widehat{g_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 1,\\
&2^{(1-{\beta})j_{2}}\|h_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s)\|_{L^{2}}+\|\widehat{h_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}+2^{\gamma j_{2}}\|\widehat{h_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}}(s)\|_{L^{1}}\lesssim 1.\end{split}$ (7.39) For $f,g\in L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})$ , $\xi\in\mathbb{R}^{3}$ , and $s\in[2^{m-1},2^{m+1}]$ let $\widetilde{G}^{\prime}_{s}(f,g)(\xi):=\varphi_{k}(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}\varphi(2^{D^{2}+\max(0,k_{1},k_{2})}\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta))\cdot\widehat{f}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{g}(\eta)\,d\eta.$ (7.40) Using also ( 7.37 ), for ( 7.36 ) it suffices to prove that, for any $s\in[2^{m-1},2^{m+1}]$ , $2^{2\beta^{2}m}2^{-\alpha k_{1}}2^{(2+\beta)m}\kappa^{1+\beta}\|\widetilde{G}^{\prime}_{s}(f,g)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 1,$ (7.41) where $f\in\{P_{k_{1}-2,k_{1}+2}g_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s),P_{k_{1}-2,k_{1}+2}h_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s)\}$ , $g\in\{P_{k_{2}-2,k_{2}+2}g_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s),P_{k_{2}-2,k_{2}+2}h_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s)\}$ . Using Lemma A.3 and ( 3.16 ) it follows that $\|\widetilde{G}^{\prime}_{s}(f,g)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\min\big(\|E^{\mu}_{s}f\|_{L^{2}}\|E^{\nu}_{s}g\|_{L^{\infty}},\|E^{\mu}_{s}f\|_{L^{\infty}}\|E^{\nu}_{s}g\|_{L^{2}}\big),$ (7.42) where $E^{\mu}_{s}f:=e^{-is\widetilde{\Lambda}_{\mu}}f$ and $E^{\nu}_{s}g:=e^{-is\widetilde{\Lambda}_{\nu}}g$ . In view of Lemma A.5 , see also ( 5.19 ), $\|E^{\mu}_{s}f\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{\beta k_{1}}2^{-m(5/4-10\beta)}2^{j_{1}(1/4-11\beta)},\qquad\|E^{\nu}_{s}g\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{-m(5/4-10\beta)}2^{j_{2}(1/4-11\beta)},$ (7.43) for $f\in\{P_{k_{1}-2,k_{1}+2}g_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s),P_{k_{1}-2,k_{1}+2}h_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s)\}$ , $g\in\{P_{k_{2}-2,k_{2}+2}g_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s),P_{k_{2}-2,k_{2}+2}h_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s)\}$ . If $|j_{1}-j_{2}|\geq 10\beta m$ then we use ( 7.42 )–( 7.43 ), together with the estimate $2^{\max(j_{1},j_{2})}\approx\kappa 2^{m}2^{-\beta^{2}m}$ and the $L^{2}$ bounds $\|f\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{2\beta k_{1}}2^{-(1-\beta)j_{1}}$ , $\|g\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-(1-\beta)j_{2}}$ , to estimate $\begin{split}\|\widetilde{G}^{\prime}_{s}(f,g)\|_{L^{2}}&\lesssim 2^{\beta k_{1}}2^{-m(5/4-10\beta)}2^{\min(j_{1},j_{2})(1/4-11\beta)}\cdot 2^{-(1-\beta)\max(j_{1},j_{2})}\\
&\lesssim 2^{\beta k_{1}}2^{-m(5/4-10\beta)}2^{-10\beta m(1/4-11\beta)}(\kappa 2^{m}2^{-\beta^{2}m})^{-3/4-10\beta}\\
&\lesssim 2^{\beta k_{1}}\kappa^{-1}2^{-2m}2^{-5\beta m/4},\end{split}$ for $f\in\{P_{k_{1}-2,k_{1}+2}g_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s),P_{k_{1}-2,k_{1}+2}h_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s)\}$ , $g\in\{P_{k_{2}-2,k_{2}+2}g_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s),P_{k_{2}-2,k_{2}+2}h_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s)\}$ . The desired bound ( 7.41 ) follows in this case. On the other hand, if $|j_{1}-j_{2}|\leq 10\beta m$ then we estimate, using ( 7.38 )–( 7.39 ) and ( 7.42 )–( 7.43 ), $\begin{split}\|\widetilde{G}^{\prime}_{s}(P_{k_{1}-2,k_{1}+2}g_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s),P_{k_{2}-2,k_{2}+2}g_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s))\|_{L^{2}}&\lesssim 2^{-m(5/4-10\beta)}2^{\min(j_{1},j_{2})(1/4-11\beta)}\cdot 2^{-(1+\beta)\max(j_{1},j_{2})}\\
&\lesssim 2^{-m(5/4-10\beta)}(\kappa 2^{m}2^{-\beta^{2}m})^{-3/4-12\beta}\\
&\lesssim\kappa^{-1}2^{-(2+2\beta)m}2^{\beta^{2}m}.\end{split}$ Moreover, for $g\in\{P_{k_{2}-2,k_{2}+2}g_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s),P_{k_{2}-2,k_{2}+2}h_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s)\}$ we estimate, using also the assumption ( 7.35 ), $\begin{split}\|\widetilde{G}^{\prime}_{s}(P_{k_{1}-2,k_{1}+2}h_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s),g)\|_{L^{2}}&\lesssim\|\widehat{h_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}}(s)\|_{L^{1}}\|g\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-\gamma j_{1}}2^{8k_{1}}2^{-(1-\beta)j_{2}}\\
&\lesssim 2^{8k_{1}}2^{16\beta m}(\kappa 2^{m})^{-(1-\beta+\gamma)}\lesssim 2^{k_{1}}2^{-m(1-17\beta+\gamma)}.\end{split}$ Finally, $\begin{split}\|\widetilde{G}^{\prime}_{s}(P_{k_{1}-2,k_{1}+2}&g_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s),P_{k_{2}-2,k_{2}+2}h_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s))\|_{L^{2}}\\
&\lesssim\min\big[\|\widehat{g_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\|\widehat{h_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}}(s)\|_{L^{1}},2^{3k_{1}/2}\|\widehat{g_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\|\widehat{h_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\big]\\
&\lesssim\min\big[2^{-(1+\beta)j_{1}}2^{-\gamma j_{2}},2^{3k_{1}/2}2^{-(1+\beta)j_{1}}2^{-(1-\beta)j_{2}}\big]\\
&\lesssim\min\big[2^{16\beta m}(\kappa 2^{m})^{-(1+\beta+\gamma)},\kappa^{3/2}2^{16\beta m}(\kappa 2^{m})^{-2}\big]\\
&\lesssim 2^{16\beta m}\min\big[2^{-(1+\gamma)m}\kappa^{-(1+\gamma)},2^{-2m}\kappa^{-1/2}\big].\end{split}$ The desired bound ( 7.41 ) follows from these last three estimates, which completes the proof in Case 1. Case 2. Assume now that $\kappa\leq 2^{k_{1}-D}\quad\text{ and }\quad\kappa\leq 2^{-m(1/3+\beta/2)}.$ (7.44) In view of ( 7.34 ), in this case it remains to prove that $2^{(1+\beta)m}2^{(1+\beta)k_{1}}\|G\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-2\beta^{2}m}.$ (7.45) As in Lemma 7.4 , see ( 7.24 )–( 7.26 ), we estimate pointwise $\begin{split}|G(\xi)|&\lesssim 2^{-2k_{1}}\cdot 2^{2k_{1}}\kappa^{3}\cdot 2^{\beta m/10}2^{-k_{1}}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{[-2^{k_{1}+D},2^{k_{1}+D}]}(|\xi|-R_{\sigma_{2}})\\
&\lesssim\kappa^{3}2^{\beta m/10}2^{-k_{1}}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{[-2^{k_{1}+D},2^{k_{1}+D}]}(|\xi|-R_{\sigma_{2}}).\end{split}$ Therefore $2^{(1+\beta)m}2^{(1+\beta)k_{1}}\|G\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{(1+5\beta/4)m}\kappa^{3},$ and the desired estimate ( 7.45 ) follows since $\kappa\leq 2^{-m(1/3+\beta/2)}$ . Case 3. Finally assume that $2^{-m(1/3+\beta/2)}\leq\kappa\leq 2^{k_{1}-D}.$ (7.46) In view of ( 7.34 ), in this case it remains to prove that $2^{(2+2\beta)m}2^{(2+2\beta)k_{1}}\|G\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-4\beta^{2}m}.$ (7.47) Step 1. We need first a suitable decomposition and an orthogonality argument. Let $\chi:\mathbb{R}\to[0,1]$ denote a smooth function supported in the interval $[-2,2]$ with the
property that $\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\chi(x-n)=1\qquad\text{ for any }x\in\mathbb{R}.$ We define the smooth function $\chi^{\prime}:\mathbb{R}^{3}\to[0,1]$ , $\chi^{\prime}(x,y,z):=\chi(x)\chi(y)\chi(z)$ . We define, for any $v\in\mathbb{Z}^{3}$ and $n\in\mathbb{Z}$ , $\begin{split}G_{v,n}(\xi):=&\chi^{\prime}(\kappa^{-1}\xi-v)\varphi_{k}(\xi)\\
&\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}\chi_{R}^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)\chi(2^{k_{1}-m}\kappa^{-1}s-n)q_{m}(s)\cdot\widehat{f_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}}(\xi-\eta,s)\widehat{f_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}}(\eta,s)\,d\eta ds.\end{split}$ (7.48) and notice that $G=\sum_{v\in\mathbb{Z}^{3}}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}G_{v,n}$ . We show now that $\|G\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim\sum_{v\in\mathbb{Z}^{3}}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\|G_{v,n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+2^{-10m}.$ (7.49) Indeed, we clearly have $\|G\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim\sum_{v\in\mathbb{Z}^{3}}\Big\|\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}G_{v,n}\Big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\sum_{v\in\mathbb{Z}^{3}}\sum_{n_{1},n_{2}\in\mathbb{Z}}|\langle G_{v,n_{1}},G_{v,n_{2}}\rangle|.$ Therefore, for ( 7.49 ) it suffices to prove that $|\langle G_{v,n_{1}},G_{v,n_{2}}\rangle|\lesssim 2^{-20m}\qquad\text{ if }v\in\mathbb{Z}^{3}\text{ and }|n_{1}-n_{2}|\geq 2^{100D}.$ (7.50) To prove this we need to estimate $|\mathcal{F}^{-1}(G_{v,n})(x)|$ . We would like to integrate by parts in the formula ( 7.48 ). Using Lemma 7.2 and Lemma A.4 (i), for $\xi=se$ , $\eta=re+\eta^{\prime}$ satisfying ( 7.7 ) with $\delta=2\kappa$ and $|\xi-\kappa v|\lesssim\kappa$ , we estimate $\begin{split}(\nabla_{\xi}\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu})(\xi,\eta)&=-(\nabla_{\eta}\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu})(\xi,\eta)+[\nabla\Lambda_{\sigma}(\xi)-\nabla\Lambda_{\sigma}(\eta)]\\
&=\lambda^{\prime}_{\sigma_{2}}(s)e-\lambda^{\prime}_{\sigma_{2}}(r^{\mu,\nu}(s))e+O_{C_{b},\varepsilon}(\kappa)\\
&=[\lambda^{\prime}_{\sigma_{2}}(\kappa|v|)-\lambda^{\prime}_{\sigma_{2}}(r^{\mu,\nu}(\kappa|v|))]\cdot v/|v|+O_{C_{b},\varepsilon}(\kappa).\end{split}$ In particular, $|\nabla_{\xi}\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)|\approx 2^{k_{1}}$ . After repeated integration by parts in $\xi$ , it follows that $\begin{split}&|\mathcal{F}^{-1}(G_{v,n})(x)|\lesssim|x+w_{n}|^{-200}\qquad\text{ if }|x+w_{n}|\geq 2^{50D}2^{m}\kappa,\\
&w_{n}:=n\kappa 2^{m-k_{1}}[\lambda^{\prime}_{\sigma_{2}}(\kappa|v|)-\lambda^{\prime}_{\sigma_{2}}(r^{\mu,\nu}(\kappa|v|))]\cdot v/|v|,\end{split}$ for any $n\in\mathbb{Z}$ . Therefore if $|n_{1}-n_{2}|\geq 2^{100D}$ then $|w_{n_{1}}-w_{n_{2}}|\geq 2^{70D}\kappa 2^{m}$ and the desired bound ( 7.50 ) follows. This completes the proof of ( 7.49 ). In view of ( 7.49 ) and Lemma 7.2 , for ( 7.47 ) it remains to prove that $2^{(2+2\beta)m}2^{(2+2\beta)k_{1}}\sum_{(v,n)\in\mathbb{Z}^{3}\times\mathbb{Z}}\|G_{v,n}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-4\beta^{2}m},$ (7.51) Let $\begin{split}G_{n}(\xi)&:=\sum_{v\in\mathbb{Z}^{3}}G_{v,n}(\xi)\\
&=\varphi_{k}(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}\chi_{R}^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)\chi(2^{k_{1}-m}\kappa^{-1}s-n)q_{m}(s)\cdot\widehat{f_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}}(\xi-\eta,s)\widehat{f_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}}(\eta,s)\,d\eta ds.\end{split}$ and 10 10 10 In some arguments that involve the use of Lemma A.3 it is necessary to pass to operators that contain ”smooth” symbols, such as the symbol $(\xi,\eta)\to\varphi(2^{D^{2}+\max(0,k_{1},k_{2})}\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta))$ in the operators $\widetilde{G}^{\prime}_{s}$ below. Lemma A.3 is not directly compatible with ”rough” symbols such as $(\xi,\eta)\to\chi_{R}^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)$ since the $L^{1}$ norm of the inverse Fourier transform of such symbols is very large. $\begin{split}G^{\prime}_{n}(\xi):=\varphi_{k}(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}&e^{is\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}\varphi(2^{D^{2}+\max(0,k_{1},k_{2})}\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta))\\
&\times\chi(2^{k_{1}-m}\kappa^{-1}s-n)q_{m}(s)\cdot\widehat{f_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}}(\xi-\eta,s)\widehat{f_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}}(\eta,s)\,d\eta ds.\end{split}$ Notice that $\sum_{v\in\mathbb{Z}^{3}}\|G_{v,n}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|G_{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},\qquad\|G_{n}-G^{\prime}_{n}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-10m},$ (7.52) for any $n\in\mathbb{Z}$ . Since $G^{\prime}_{n}\equiv 0$ unless $n\in[2^{k_{1}-4}\kappa^{-1},2^{k_{1}+4}\kappa^{-1}]$ , for ( 7.51 ) it suffices to prove that $\sup_{n\in[2^{k_{1}-4}\kappa^{-1},2^{k_{1}+4}\kappa^{-1}]}2^{(1+\beta)m}2^{(1+\beta)k_{1}}2^{k_{1}/2}\kappa^{-1/2}\|G^{\prime}_{n}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-2\beta^{2}m}.$ (7.53) For $f,g\in L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})$ , $\xi\in\mathbb{R}^{3}$ , and $s\in[2^{m-1},2^{m+1}]$ let, as in ( 7.40 ), $\widetilde{G}^{\prime}_{s}(f,g)(\xi)=\varphi_{k}(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}\varphi(2^{D^{2}+\max(0,k_{1},k_{2})}\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta))\cdot\widehat{f}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{g}(\eta)\,d\eta.$ The left-hand side of ( 7.53 ) is dominated by $2^{(1+\beta)m}2^{(1+\beta)k_{1}}2^{k_{1}/2}\kappa^{-1/2}\cdot 2^{m}\kappa 2^{-k_{1}}\sup_{s\in[2^{m-1},2^{m+1}]}\|\widetilde{G}^{\prime}_{s}(f_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu},f_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu})\|_{L^{2}}.$ Therefore, it remains to prove that $2^{(2+\beta)m}2^{(1/2+\beta)k_{1}}\kappa^{1/2}\sup_{s\in[2^{m-1},2^{m+1}]}\|\widetilde{G}^{\prime}_{s}(f_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s),f_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s))\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-2\beta^{2}m}.$ (7.54) Step 2. We decompose $\widetilde{\varphi}_{j_{1}}^{(k_{1})}\cdot P_{k_{1}}f_{\mu}(s)=2^{-\alpha k_{1}}[g_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s)+h_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s)]$ and $\widetilde{\varphi}_{j_{2}}^{(k_{2})}\cdot P_{k_{2}}f_{\nu}(s)=[g_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s)+h_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s)]$ as in ( 7.38 )–( 7.39 ). In this proof we will also need the stronger bounds ( 4.8 ) on the functions $h_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s)$ and $h_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s)$ , $\begin{split}&2^{\gamma j_{1}}\sup_{R\in[2^{-j_{1}},2^{k_{1}}],\,\xi_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}R^{-2}\|\widehat{h_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}}(s)\|_{L^{1}(B(\xi_{0},R))}\lesssim 2^{10k_{1}},\\
&2^{\gamma j_{2}}\sup_{R\in[2^{-j_{2}},2^{k_{2}}],\,\xi_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}R^{-2}\|\widehat{h_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}}(s)\|_{L^{1}(B(\xi_{0},R))}\lesssim 1,\end{split}$ (7.55) and the support properties ( 4.7 ). Recall the $L^{2}$ bounds $\begin{split}&\|g_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-(1+\beta)j_{1}},\qquad\|h_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{8k_{1}}2^{-(1-\beta)j_{1}},\\
&\|g_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-(1+\beta)j_{2}},\qquad\|h_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-(1-\beta)j_{2}}.\end{split}$ (7.56) With $E^{\mu}_{s}f=e^{-is\widetilde{\Lambda}_{\mu}}f$ and $E^{\nu}_{s}g=e^{-is\widetilde{\Lambda}_{\nu}}g$ as in the proof in Case 1, we use the kernel bounds ( A.49 ), ( A.37 ), and ( A.42 ) (as in the proof of Lemma A.5 ) to conclude that $\begin{split}&\|E^{\mu}_{s}P_{[k_{1}-2,k_{1}+2]}(g_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s))\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{k_{1}/2}2^{-3m/2}2^{j_{1}(1/2-\beta)},\\
&\|E^{\mu}_{s}P_{[k_{1}-2,k_{1}+2]}(h_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s))\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{8k_{1}}\min[2^{-3m/2}2^{j_{1}(1/2+\beta)},2^{-\gamma j_{1}}]\\
&\|E^{\nu}_{s}P_{[k_{2}-2,k_{2}+2]}(g_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s))\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{-3m/2}2^{j_{2}(1/2-\beta)},\\
&\|E^{\nu}_{s}P_{[k_{2}-2,k_{2}+2]}(h_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s))\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\min[2^{-3m/2}2^{j_{2}(1/2+\beta)},2^{-\gamma j_{2}}],\end{split}$ (7.57) for any $s\in[2^{m-1},2^{m+1}]$ . We combine these bounds and Lemma A.3 . It follows from ( 7.57 ) that $\|Ef_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{-3m/2}2^{j_{1}(1/2+\beta)}$ and $\|Ef_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{-3m/2}2^{j_{2}(1/2+\beta)}$ . Recalling that $2^{\max(j_{1},j_{2})}\approx\kappa 2^{m}2^{-\beta^{2}m}$ (see ( 7.32 )), we have $\begin{split}\|\widetilde{G}^{\prime}_{s}(f_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s),f_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s))\|_{L^{2}}&\lesssim 2^{-3m/2}2^{\min(j_{1},j_{2})(1/2+\beta)}\cdot 2^{-(1-\beta)\max(j_{1},j_{2})}\\
&\lesssim 2^{-|j_{1}-j_{2}|(1/2+\beta)}2^{-3m/2}2^{(-1/2+2\beta)\max(j_{1},j_{2})}\\
&\lesssim 2^{-|j_{1}-j_{2}|(1/2+\beta)}2^{-2m}\kappa^{-1/2}\cdot 2^{\beta^{2}m}2^{2\beta m},\end{split}$ for any $s\in[2^{m-1},2^{m+1}]$ . The desired bound ( 7.54 ) follows if $2^{-|j_{1}-j_{2}|}2^{k_{1}}\leq 2^{-6\beta m}$ . It remains to prove ( 7.54 ) in the case $2^{|j_{1}-j_{2}|}2^{-k_{1}}\leq 2^{6\beta m}.$ (7.58) We start by using the bounds ( 7.56 )–( 7.57 ) more carefully. We estimate $\|\widetilde{G}^{\prime}_{s}(f,g)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-3m/2}2^{\min(j_{1},j_{2})(1/2-\beta)}\cdot 2^{-(1+\beta)\max(j_{1},j_{2})}\lesssim 2^{-(2+2\beta)m}\kappa^{-1/2}\kappa^{-2\beta}2^{\beta^{2}m},$ if $(f,g)=\big(P_{[k_{1}-2,k_{1}+2]}(g_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s)),P_{[k_{2}-2,k_{2}+2]}(g_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s))\big)$ . This is consistent with the desired bound ( 7.54 ), if we recall that $\kappa^{-1}\lesssim 2^{m/3+\beta m/2}$ (see ( 7.46 )). Therefore it remains the prove that $2^{(2+\beta)m}2^{k_{1}/2}\kappa^{1/2}\sup_{s\in[2^{m-1},2^{m+1}]}\|\widetilde{G}^{\prime}_{s}(f,g)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-2\beta^{2}m},$ (7.59) if ( 7.58 ) holds, and $\begin{split}(f,g)\in\Big\{&(P_{[k_{1}-2,k_{1}+2]}(g_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s)),P_{[k_{2}-2,k_{2}+2]}(h_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s))\big),\\
&\big(P_{[k_{1}-2,k_{1}+2]}(h_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s)),P_{[k_{2}-2,k_{2}+2]}(g_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s))\big),\\
&\big(P_{[k_{1}-2,k_{1}+2]}(h_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s)),P_{[k_{2}-2,k_{2}+2]}(h_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s))\big)\Big\}.\end{split}$ (7.60) One could try arguing as before: recalling that $\gamma=3/2-4\beta$ and using ( 7.58 ), for $(f,g)$ as in ( 7.60 ) we estimate $\begin{split}\|\widetilde{G}^{\prime}_{s}(f,g)\|_{L^{2}}&\lesssim 2^{-\gamma\min(j_{1},j_{2})}\cdot 2^{-(1-\beta)\max(j_{1},j_{2})}\\
&\lesssim 2^{\gamma|j_{1}-j_{2}|}2^{-(\gamma+1-\beta)\max(j_{1},j_{2})}\\
&\lesssim 2^{-5m/2+15\beta m}\kappa^{-5/2+5\beta}2^{3\beta^{2}m},\end{split}$ Therefore the left-hand side of ( 7.59 ) is dominated by $C2^{k_{1}/2}2^{(2+\beta)m}\kappa^{1/2}\cdot 2^{3\beta^{2}m}2^{-5m/2+15\beta m}\kappa^{-5/2+5\beta}\lesssim 2^{3\beta^{2}m}2^{-m(1/2-16\beta)}\kappa^{-(2-5\beta)}.$ The desired bound ( 7.59 ) follows if $\kappa^{-1}$ is sufficiently small, say $\kappa^{-1}\leq 2^{m/6}$ , but not in the full range $\kappa^{-1}\leq 2^{m(1/3+\beta/2)}$ (see ( 7.46 )). To cover the full range we need an additional argument that uses the stronger bounds ( 7.55 ). Step 3. We prove now ( 7.59 ). We reinsert first the cutoff function $\chi_{R}^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}$ , i.e., we define $\begin{split}&\widetilde{G}^{\prime\prime}_{s}(f,g)(\xi):=\varphi_{k}(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}\chi_{R}^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)\cdot\widehat{f}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{g}(\eta)\,d\eta.\\
&\chi_{R}^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)=\varphi(2^{D^{2}+\max(0,k_{1},k_{2})}\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta))\varphi(|\Xi^{\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)|/\kappa),\end{split}$ (7.61) where $(f,g)$ are as in ( 7.60 ). As before, integrating by parts in $\eta$ , we notice that $\|\widetilde{G}^{\prime\prime}_{s}(f,g)-\widetilde{G}^{\prime}_{s}(f,g)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-10m}$ . Then we decompose $\begin{split}&\widetilde{G}^{\prime\prime}_{s}(f,g)=\sum_{v\in\mathbb{Z}^{3}}\widetilde{G}^{\prime\prime}_{v,s}(f,g),\\
&\widetilde{G}^{\prime\prime}_{v,s}(f,g):=\chi^{\prime}(\kappa^{-1}\xi-v)\varphi_{k}(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}\chi_{R}^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)\cdot\widehat{f}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{g}(\eta)\,d\eta,\end{split}$ (7.62) where $\chi^{\prime}$ is as before. For ( 7.59 ) it remains to prove that $2^{(4+2\beta)m}2^{k_{1}}\kappa\sum_{v\in\mathbb{Z}^{3}}\|\widetilde{G}^{\prime\prime}_{v,s}(f,g)\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-4\beta^{2}m},$ (7.63) for any $s\in[2^{m-1},2^{m+1}]$ and $(f,g)$ as in ( 7.60 ). In view of Lemma 7.2 the variables in the definition of the function $\widetilde{G}^{\prime\prime}_{v,s}(f,g)$ are naturally restricted as follows: $\begin{split}&v\in\mathbb{Z}^{3},\qquad\big|\kappa|v|-R_{\sigma_{2}}\big|\lesssim_{C_{b},\varepsilon}2^{k_{1}}\\
&\xi=a\widehat{v}+\xi^{\prime},\qquad\xi^{\prime}\cdot\widehat{v}=0,\qquad|\xi^{\prime}|\lesssim_{C_{b},\varepsilon}\kappa,\qquad\big|a-\kappa|v|\big|\lesssim_{C_{b},\varepsilon}\kappa,\\
&\xi-\eta=b\widehat{v}+\theta^{\prime},\qquad\theta^{\prime}\cdot\widehat{v}=0,\qquad|\theta^{\prime}|\lesssim_{C_{b},\varepsilon}2^{k_{1}}\kappa,\qquad\big|b-(\kappa|v|-r^{\mu,\nu}(\kappa|v|))\big|\lesssim_{C_{b},\varepsilon}\kappa,\end{split}$ where $\widehat{v}=v/|v|$ . More precisely, for any $v$ fixed we define the functions $f^{v}$ and $g^{v}$ by the formulas $\begin{split}&\widehat{f^{v}}(\theta):=\varphi[|\theta^{\prime}|/(\kappa 2^{k_{1}+D})]\varphi\big[[\rho-\kappa|v|+r^{\mu,\nu}(\kappa|v|)]/(\kappa 2^{D})\big]\cdot\widehat{f}(\theta),\\
&\widehat{g^{v}}(\theta):=\varphi(|\theta^{\prime}|/(\kappa 2^{D}))\varphi\big[[\rho-r^{\mu,\nu}(\kappa|v|)]/(\kappa 2^{D})\big]\cdot\widehat{g}(\theta),\end{split}$ (7.64) where $\theta=\rho\widehat{v}+\theta^{\prime}$ , $\rho\in\mathbb{R}$ , $\theta^{\prime}\cdot\widehat{v}=0$ . In view of Lemma 7.2 and ( 7.6 ), the functions $\widehat{f^{v}}$ (respectively $\widehat{g^{v}}$ ) have essentially pairwise disjoint supports, i.e., $\sum_{v\in\mathbb{Z}^{3}}\|\widehat{f^{v}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},\qquad\sum_{v\in\mathbb{Z}^{3}}\|\widehat{g^{v}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim 2^{-k_{1}}\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.$ (7.65) Moreover, they suffice to determine the functions $\widetilde{G}^{\prime\prime}_{v,s}(f,g)$ , i.e., $\widetilde{G}^{\prime\prime}_{v,s}(f,g)(\xi)=\chi^{\prime}(\kappa^{-1}\xi-v)\varphi_{k}(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}\chi_{R}^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)\cdot\widehat{f^{v}}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{g^{v}}(\eta)\,d\eta.$ We use ( 7.55 ), ( 7.56 ), and ( 7.65 ). For $(f,g)=\big(P_{[k_{1}-2,k_{1}+2]}(g_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s)),P_{[k_{2}-2,k_{2}+2]}(h_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s))\big)$ or $(f,g)=\big(P_{[k_{1}-2,k_{1}+2]}(h_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s)),P_{[k_{2}-2,k_{2}+2]}(h_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s))\big)$ , we estimate $\sum_{v\in\mathbb{Z}^{3}}\|\widetilde{G}^{\prime\prime}_{v,s}(f,g)\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim\sum_{v\in\mathbb{Z}^{3}}\|\widehat{f^{v}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\widehat{g^{v}}\|_{L^{1}}^{2}\lesssim\|\widehat{f}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\sup_{v\in\mathbb{Z}^{3}}\|\widehat{g^{v}}\|_{L^{1}}^{2}\lesssim 2^{-2j_{1}+2\beta j_{1}}2^{-2\gamma j_{2}}\kappa^{4}.$ For $(f,g)=\big(P_{[k_{1}-2,k_{1}+2]}(h_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu}(s)),P_{[k_{2}-2,k_{2}+2]}(g_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu}(s))\big)$ we estimate $\sum_{v\in\mathbb{Z}^{3}}\|\widetilde{G}^{\prime\prime}_{v,s}(f,g)\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim\sum_{v\in\mathbb{Z}^{3}}\|\widehat{f^{v}}\|_{L^{1}}^{2}\|\widehat{g^{v}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim 2^{-k_{1}}\|\widehat{g}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\sup_{v\in\mathbb{Z}^{3}}\|\widehat{f^{v}}\|_{L^{1}}^{2}\lesssim 2^{-2j_{2}+2\beta j_{2}}2^{-2\gamma j_{1}}\kappa^{4}.$ Therefore, using also the assumption ( 7.58 ), the left-hand side of ( 7.63 ) is dominated by $C2^{(4+2\beta)m}\kappa\cdot\kappa^{4}2^{-2\gamma\min(j_{1},j_{2})}2^{-(2-2\beta)\max(j_{1},j_{2})}\lesssim 2^{(4+2\beta)m}\kappa^{5}2^{2\gamma|j_{1}-j_{2}|}2^{-(2\gamma+2-2\beta)\max(j_{1},j_{2})}\lesssim 2^{-\beta m},$ and the desired bound ( 7.63 ) follows. This completes the proof of the lemma.
∎ 8. Proof of Proposition 4.3 , IV: Case C resonant interactions Proposition 8.1 . Assume that $(k,j),(k_{1},j_{1}),(k_{2},j_{2})\in\mathcal{J}$ , $m\in[1,L]\cap\mathbb{Z}$ , $\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}\in\in\mathcal{T}_{C}=\{\Phi^{i;i+,i+},\Phi^{i;i+,i-},\Phi^{i;i-,i-},\Phi^{i;e+,e-},\Phi^{i;e+,b-},\Phi^{i;e-,b+},\Phi^{i;b+,b-}\},$ (8.1) and $\begin{split}&-9m/10\leq k_{1},k_{2}\leq j/N_{0}^{\prime},\qquad\max(j_{1},j_{2})\leq(1-\beta/10)m+k,\qquad m\geq-k(1+\beta^{2}),\\
&\beta m/2+N_{0}^{\prime}k_{+}+D^{2}\leq j\leq m+D,\qquad k\leq-D/4.\end{split}$ (8.2) Then there is $\kappa\in(0,1]$ , $\kappa\geq\max\big(2^{(\beta^{2}m-m)/2}2^{-\min(k_{1},k_{2},0)/2}2^{-D/2},2^{\beta^{2}m-m}2^{\max(j_{1},j_{2})}\big)$ , such that $2^{k}\big\|\widetilde{\varphi}^{(k)}_{j}\cdot P_{k}R_{m,\kappa}^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(f_{k_{1},j_{1}}^{\mu},f_{k_{2},j_{2}}^{\nu})\big\|_{B^{1}_{k,j}}\lesssim 2^{-2\beta^{4}m}.$ (8.3) The rest of the section is concerned with the proof of Proposition 8.1 . Many of the easier cases can be handled using the following lemma: Lemma 8.2 . (i) With the hypothesis in Proposition 8.1 , assume in addition that either $\Big\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\left\{\frac{1}{\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}\varphi_{[k-4,k+4]}(\xi)\varphi_{[k_{1}-4,k_{1}+4]}(\xi-\eta)\varphi_{[k_{2}-4,k_{2}+4]}(\eta)\right\}\Big\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}\lesssim 2^{-k}2^{6\max(k_{1},k_{2},0)},$ (8.4) or that $\begin{split}&10\beta k\leq\max(k_{1},k_{2})\leq-D/100,\qquad\max(j_{1},j_{2})\leq 3\beta m,\\
&\Big\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\left\{\frac{1}{\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}\varphi_{[k-4,k+4]}(\xi)\varphi_{[k_{1}-4,k_{1}+4]}(\xi-\eta)\varphi_{[k_{2}-4,k_{2}+4]}(\eta)\right\}\Big\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}\lesssim 2^{-k}2^{m/3},\\
&\Big\|\frac{1}{\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}\varphi_{[k-4,k+4]}(\xi)\varphi_{[k_{1}-4,k_{1}+4]}(\xi-\eta)\varphi_{[k_{2}-4,k_{2}+4]}(\eta)\Big\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}\lesssim 2^{-k-k_{1}-k_{2}}.\end{split}$ (8.5) Then, for any $\kappa\in(0,1]$ , $\kappa\geq\max\big(2^{(\beta^{2}m-m)/2}2^{-\min(k_{1},k_{2},0)/2}2^{-D/2},2^{\beta^{2}m-m}2^{\max(j_{1},j_{2})}\big)$ , $2^{k}\|\widetilde{\varphi}^{(k)}_{j}\cdot P_{k}R_{m,\kappa}^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}},f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}})\|_{B^{1}_{k,j}}\lesssim 2^{-2\beta^{4}m}.$ (8.6) (ii) The inequality ( 8.4 ) holds if $|\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)|\gtrsim 2^{\widetilde{k}}+2^{\widetilde{k_{1}}}+2^{\widetilde{k_{2}}}$ (8.7) for all $(\xi,\eta)\in\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}$ satisfying $|\xi|\in[2^{k-6},2^{k+6}]$ , $|\xi-\eta|\in[2^{k_{1}-6},2^{k_{1}+6}]$ , $|\eta|\in[2^{k_{2}-6},2^{k_{2}+6}]$ . Proof of Lemma 8.2 . (i) The proof is similar to the bound on $N^{1;\sigma;\mu,\nu}_{m}$ in Lemma 5.8 . In view of ( 5.67 ) it suffices to prove that $2^{k}\|\widetilde{\varphi}^{(k)}_{j}\cdot P_{k}T_{m}^{i;\mu,\nu}(f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}},f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}})\|_{B^{1}_{k,j}}\lesssim 2^{-2\beta^{4}m}.$ (8.8) After integration by parts in $s$ in ( 5.8 ), we obtain that $\begin{split}2^{k}\mathcal{F}[T_{m}^{i;\mu,\nu}(f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}},f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}})]&=i\left[T_{1}+T_{2}+T_{3}\right],\\
T_{1}(\xi)&:=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}\frac{2^{k}}{\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}q^{\prime}_{m}(s)\cdot\widehat{f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}}(\xi-\eta,s)\widehat{f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}}(\eta,s)\,d\eta ds,\\
T_{2}(\xi)&:=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}\frac{2^{k}}{\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}q_{m}(s)\cdot(\partial_{s}\widehat{f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}})(\xi-\eta,s)\widehat{f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}}(\eta,s)\,d\eta ds,\\
T_{3}(\xi)&:=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}\frac{2^{k}}{\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}q_{m}(s)\cdot\widehat{f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}}(\xi-\eta,s)(\partial_{s}\widehat{f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}})(\eta,s)\,d\eta ds.\end{split}$ (8.9) Recall Definition 4.1 . We first show that $2^{(1/2-\beta+\alpha)k}\cdot 2^{k}\|\varphi_{k}\cdot\mathcal{F}T_{m}^{i;\mu,\nu}(f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}},f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}})\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{-2\beta^{4}m}.$ (8.10) Indeed, using Cauchy-Schwartz inequality, ( 5.17 ), ( 5.20 ), and either ( 8.4 ) or ( 8.5 ), we see that, letting $\Lambda:=2^{-20\beta k}+2^{6\max(k_{1},k_{2},0)}$ , $\begin{split}\|\varphi_{k}\cdot T_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}&\lesssim 2^{m}\Lambda\sup_{s\in[2^{m-2},2^{m+2}]}\|(\partial_{s}\widehat{f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}}(s))\|_{L^{2}}\|\widehat{f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\Lambda 2^{-\beta m}2^{-10\max(k_{1},k_{2},0)},\\
\|\varphi_{k}\cdot T_{3}\|_{L^{\infty}}&\lesssim 2^{m}\Lambda\sup_{s\in[2^{m-2},2^{m+2}]}\|\widehat{f}^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\|(\partial_{s}\widehat{f}^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}(s))\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\Lambda 2^{-\beta m}2^{-10\max(k_{1},k_{2},0)},\end{split}$ and this gives acceptable contributions. Proceeding as above, using ( 5.18 ) we get $\|\varphi_{k}\cdot T_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\Lambda 2^{-(1-\beta)(j_{1}+j_{2})}(1+2^{k_{1}})^{-10}(1+2^{k_{2}})^{-10}.$ Therefore, this gives an acceptable contribution to ( 8.10 ) unless $|k|+|k_{1}|+|k_{2}|+j_{1}+j_{2}\leq\beta^{2}m.$ (8.11) Now, assuming that ( 8.11 ) holds, we can strengthen the $L^{\infty}$ bound. Indeed, we decompose $\begin{split}T_{1}(\xi)&=T_{1;1}(\xi)+T_{1;2}(\xi)\\
T_{1;1}(\xi)&:=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}\frac{2^{k}}{\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}\varphi(\delta^{-1}|\Xi^{\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)|)q^{\prime}_{m}(s)\cdot\widehat{f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}}(\xi-\eta,s)\widehat{f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}}(\eta,s)\,d\eta ds,\\
T_{1;2}(\xi)&:=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}\frac{2^{k}}{\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}(1-\varphi(\delta^{-1}|\Xi^{\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta))|)q^{\prime}_{m}(s)\cdot\widehat{f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}}(\xi-\eta,s)\widehat{f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}}(\eta,s)\,d\eta ds,\\
\end{split}$ with $\delta=2^{-m/3}$ . Applying Lemma A.2 with $K\approx 2^{2m/3}$ , $\epsilon\approx 2^{-m/3}$ , it is easy to see that $\|\varphi_{k}\cdot T_{1;2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{-10m}$ if ( 8.11 ) holds, which is clearly sufficient. On the other hand, we observe that $\begin{split}|\Xi^{\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)|&\gtrsim|\nabla\widetilde{\Lambda}_{\nu}(\eta)|\cdot\min(\big|(\xi-\eta)/|\xi-\eta|-\eta/|\eta|\big|,\big||(\xi-\eta)/|\xi-\eta|+\eta/|\eta|\big|)\\
&\gtrsim 2^{-\beta m}\min(\big|(\xi-\eta)/|\xi-\eta|-\eta/|\eta|\big|,\big|(\xi-\eta)/|\xi-\eta|+\eta/|\eta|\big|)\end{split}$ Consequently if $|\xi|\in[2^{k-2},2^{k+2}]$ , $|\xi-\eta|\in[2^{k_{1}-2}2^{k_{1}+2}]$ , $|\eta|\in[2^{k_{2}-2},2^{k_{2}+2}]$ , and $|\Xi^{\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta))|\lesssim 2^{-m/3}$ then $\min(\big|(\xi-\eta)/|\xi-\eta|-\eta/|\eta|\big|,\big|(\xi-\eta)/|\xi-\eta|+\eta/|\eta|\big|)\lesssim 2^{-m/4}.$ A simple estimate using the $L^{\infty}$ bounds in ( 5.17 ) then gives $\|\varphi_{k}\cdot T_{1;1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{-m/6}$ , which suffices to finish the proof of ( 8.10 ). To finish the proof of ( 8.8 ), it suffices to prove that $2^{(1+\alpha)k}2^{(1+\beta)m}\|P_{k}T_{m}^{i;\mu,\nu}(f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}},f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}})\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-2\beta^{4}m}.$ (8.12) Assume first that ( 8.4 ) holds. Then we use the $L^{2}$ bounds $\begin{split}&\|f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim(2^{\alpha k_{1}}+2^{10k_{1}})^{-1}2^{-(1-\beta)j_{1}}2^{2\beta k_{1}},\\
&\|f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim(2^{\alpha k_{2}}+2^{10k_{2}})^{-1}2^{-(1-\beta)j_{2}}2^{2\beta k_{2}},\\
&\|(\partial_{s}f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}})(s)\|_{L^{2}}+\|(\partial_{s}f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}})(s)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-m(1+\beta)},\end{split}$ (8.13) and the $L^{\infty}$ bounds $\begin{split}&\|Ef^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\min(2^{-6k_{1}},2^{(1/2-\alpha-\beta)k_{1}})2^{-m(1+\beta)},\\
&\|Ef^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{-m(5/4-10\beta)}2^{j_{1}(1/4-11\beta)},\\
&\|Ef^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\min(2^{-6k_{2}},2^{(1/2-\alpha-\beta)k_{2}})2^{-m(1+\beta)},\\
&\|Ef^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{-m(5/4-10\beta)}2^{j_{2}(1/4-11\beta)},\end{split}$ (8.14) for any $s\in[2^{m-1},2^{m+1}]$ , see ( 5.17 )–( 5.20 ). Using the assumption ( 8.4 ) and Lemma A.3 , it follows that $\begin{split}&2^{\alpha k}2^{(1+\beta)m}\|P_{k}T_{1}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{(1+\beta)m}\cdot 2^{-m(5/4-10\beta)}2^{\min(j_{1},j_{2})(1/4-11\beta)}2^{-(1-\beta)\max(j_{1},j_{2})}\lesssim 2^{-\beta m},\\
&2^{\alpha k}2^{(1+\beta)m}\|P_{k}T_{2}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{(1+\beta)m}\cdot 2^{m}2^{-m(1+\beta)}2^{-m(1+\beta)}\lesssim 2^{-\beta m},\\
&2^{\alpha k}2^{(1+\beta)m}\|P_{k}T_{3}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{(1+\beta)m}\cdot 2^{m}2^{-m(1+\beta)}2^{-m(1+\beta)}\lesssim 2^{-\beta m},\end{split}$ and the desired bound ( 8.12 ) follows. Assume now that ( 8.5 ) holds. We estimate as before, using however the stronger $L^{\infty}$ bounds $\begin{split}&\|Ef^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{-m(3/2-10\beta)}2^{j_{1}(1/2-11\beta)},\\
&\|Ef^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{-m(3/2-10\beta)}2^{j_{2}(1/2-11\beta)},\end{split}$ (8.15) see ( A.21 ) and ( A.23 ). Then we estimate, using ( 8.13 ), ( 8.15 ), and Lemma A.3 , $\begin{split}2^{\alpha k}2^{(1+\beta)m}\|P_{k}T_{1}\|_{L^{2}}&\lesssim 2^{(1+\beta)m}\cdot 2^{m/3}\cdot 2^{-m(3/2-10\beta)}2^{\min(j_{1},j_{2})(1/2-11\beta)}2^{-(1-\beta)\max(j_{1},j_{2})}\\
&\lesssim 2^{m(4/3-3/2+12\beta)}\lesssim 2^{-\beta^{2}m}.\end{split}$ Similarly, we estimate, for $l\in\{2,3\}$ $\begin{split}2^{\alpha k}2^{(1+\beta)m}\|P_{k}T_{l}\|_{L^{2}}&\lesssim 2^{(1+\beta)m}\cdot 2^{m}2^{m/3}2^{-m(1+\beta)}2^{-m(3/2-10\beta)}2^{\max(j_{1},j_{2})(1/2-11\beta)}\\
&\lesssim 2^{m(4/3-3/2+12\beta)}\lesssim 2^{-\beta^{2}m}.\end{split}$ The desired bound ( 8.12 ) follows from the last two estimates. (ii) Without loss of generality we may assume that $k_{1}\geq k_{2}$ . Let $K(x,y):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{ix\cdot\xi}e^{iy\cdot\eta}\frac{1}{\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}\varphi_{[k-4,k+4]}(\xi)\varphi_{[k_{1}-4,k_{1}+4]}(\xi-\eta)\varphi_{[k_{2}-4,k_{2}+4]}(\eta)\,d\xi d\eta.$ We can integrate by parts to prove suitable estimates on the kernel $K$ . We use the general formula $\partial^{a}_{\xi}\partial^{b}_{\eta}\frac{1}{\Phi}=\sum_{n\leq|a|+|b|,\,|a_{1}|+\ldots+|a_{n}|=|a|,\,|b_{1}|+\ldots+|b_{n}|=|b|}c_{a_{1},\ldots a_{n},b_{1},\ldots b_{n}}\frac{1}{\Phi}\frac{\partial^{a_{1}}_{\xi}\partial^{b_{1}}_{\eta}\Phi}{\Phi}\ldots\frac{\partial^{a_{n}}_{\xi}\partial^{b_{n}}_{\eta}\Phi}{\Phi},$ (8.16) for any multi-indices $a$ and $b$ , which follows easily by induction. It follows from ( 8.7 ) that $|K(x,y)|(1+2^{\widetilde{k}}|x|)^{4}(1+2^{\widetilde{k}_{2}}|y|)^{4}\lesssim 2^{3k}2^{3k_{2}}\left[2^{\widetilde{k}}+2^{\widetilde{k_{1}}}+2^{\widetilde{k_{2}}}\right]^{-1}\lesssim 2^{-\widetilde{k}_{1}}2^{3(k+k_{2})}$ for any $x,y\in\mathbb{R}^{3}$ . Therefore $\|K\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}\lesssim 2^{-\widetilde{k}_{1}}2^{6\max(0,k_{1})}$ as desired.
∎ We will also need the following result whose proof is identical to the one of the first case of $(i)$ above since we can always use Lemma A.1 to pass from $f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}$ to $Q_{1}f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}$ and from $f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}$ to $Q_{2}f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}$ . Lemma 8.3 . Let $q_{0},q_{1},q_{2}\in\mathcal{S}^{100}$ and define the operators $Q_{0}$ , $Q_{1}$ and $Q_{2}$ by $\mathcal{F}\left[Q_{j}f\right](\xi)=q_{j}(\xi)\widehat{f}(\xi).$ With the hypothesis in Proposition 8.1 , assume in addition that $\Big\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\left\{\frac{q_{0}(\xi)q_{1}(\xi-\eta)q_{2}(\eta)}{\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}\varphi_{[k-4,k+4]}(\xi)\varphi_{[k_{1}-4,k_{1}+4]}(\xi-\eta)\varphi_{[k_{2}-4,k_{2}+4]}(\eta)\right\}\Big\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}\lesssim 2^{-k}2^{6\max(k_{1},k_{2},0)},$ (8.17) then, for any $\kappa\in(0,1]$ , $\kappa\geq\max\big(2^{(\beta^{2}m-m)/2}2^{-\min(k_{1},k_{2},0)/2}2^{-D/2},2^{\beta^{2}m-m}2^{\max(j_{1},j_{2})}\big)$ , $2^{k}\|\widetilde{\varphi}^{(k)}_{j}\cdot P_{k}R_{m,\kappa}^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(Q_{1}f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}},Q_{2}f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}})\|_{B^{1}_{k,j}}\lesssim 2^{-2\beta^{4}m}.$ (8.18) 8.1. Proof of Proposition 8.1 We divide the proof in several cases. We consider first the easier phases. Lemma 8.4 . The bound ( 8.3 ) holds if ( 8.2 ) holds and, in addition, $\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}\in\in\{\Phi^{i;i-,i-},\Phi^{i;e+,e-},\Phi^{i;e+,b-},\Phi^{i;e-,b+},\Phi^{i;b+,b-}\},$ (8.19) with $\kappa=\max\big(2^{(\beta^{2}m-m)/2}2^{-\min(k_{1},k_{2},0)/2}2^{-D/2},2^{\beta^{2}m-m}2^{\max(j_{1},j_{2})}\big)$ . Proof of Lemma 8.4 . The condition ( 8.7 ) is clearly satisfied if $\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}=\Phi^{i;i-,i-}$ . This condition is also satisfied if $\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}\in\in\{\Phi^{i;e+,e-},\Phi^{i;e+,b-},\Phi^{i;e-,b+},\Phi^{i;b+,b-}\}\qquad\text{ and }\qquad 2\max(k_{1},k_{2})\leq k-D/10.$ Indeed, in this case $|\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)|\geq\Lambda_{i}(\xi)-|\Lambda_{\sigma_{1}}(\xi-\eta)-\Lambda_{\sigma_{2}}(\eta)|\gtrsim|\xi|-C_{C_{b},\varepsilon}(|\xi-\eta|^{2}+|\eta|^{2})\gtrsim 2^{k}.$ The desired bound ( 8.3 ) then follows from Lemma 8.2 in these cases. It remains to prove the proposition in the case $\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}\in\in\{\Phi^{i;e+,e-},\Phi^{i;e+,b-},\Phi^{i;e-,b+},\Phi^{i;b+,b-}\}\qquad\text{ and }\qquad k\leq 2\max(k_{1},k_{2})+D/10.$ (8.20) In this case, since $k\leq-D/4$ from ( 8.2 ), we remark that $k<\min(k_{1},k_{2})-20$ , $|k_{1}-k_{2}|\leq 8$ (otherwise, we would have $k\geq\max(k_{1},k_{2})-4$ , which is incompatible with ( 8.20 ) and $k\leq-D/4$ ) and that $\begin{split}|\Xi^{\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)|\gtrsim_{C_{b},\varepsilon}\begin{cases}|\xi|(1+|\xi-\eta|+|\eta|)^{-3}&\quad\hbox{if }\sigma_{1}=\sigma_{2},\\
(|\eta|+|\xi-\eta|)(1+|\xi-\eta|+|\eta|)^{-3}&\quad\hbox{if }\sigma_{1}\neq\sigma_{2}.\end{cases}\end{split}$ (8.21) These inequalities follow from Lemma A.4 . Consequently, we see that if $\begin{split}k&\geq 2\beta m-m/2-\min(k_{1},k_{2},0)/2\quad\hbox{if}\quad\sigma_{1}=\sigma_{2},\\
\max(k_{1},k_{2})&\geq 2\beta m-m/2-\min(k_{1},k_{2},0)/2\quad\hbox{if}\quad\sigma_{1}\neq\sigma_{2},\end{split}$ (8.22) then $P_{k}R^{i;\mu,\nu}_{m,\kappa}=0$ , since $\kappa=\max(2^{-(1-\beta^{2})m/2}2^{-\min(k_{1},k_{2},0)/2}2^{-D/2},2^{\max(j_{1},j_{2})-(1-\beta^{2})m})$ . The desired bound ( 8.3 ) becomes trivial in this case. Independently, using Lemma A.3 and ( A.37 ), ( A.42 ), we directly see that $\begin{split}\|P_{k}T_{m}^{i;\mu,\nu}&(f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}},f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}})\|_{L^{2}}\\
&\lesssim 2^{m}\sup_{s\in[2^{m-4},2^{m+4}]}\min(\|Ef^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}\|f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}(s)\|_{L^{2}},\|f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\|Ef^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}})\\
&\lesssim 2^{m}2^{-3m/2},\\
\end{split}$ from which we deduce that $\begin{split}2^{(1+\alpha)k}2^{(1+\beta)j}\|P_{k}T_{m}^{i;\mu,\nu}(f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}},f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}})\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{(1+\alpha)k}2^{(1/2+\beta)m}.\end{split}$ In addition $\begin{split}2^{(3/2-\beta+\alpha)k}\|\mathcal{F}P_{k}T_{m}^{i;\mu,\nu}(f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}},f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}})\|_{L^{\infty}}&\lesssim 2^{(3/2-\beta+\alpha)k}2^{m}\sup_{s\in[2^{m-4},2^{m+4}]}\|f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\|f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\\
&\lesssim 2^{(3/2-\beta+\alpha)k}2^{m}.\end{split}$ Therefore $\begin{split}2^{k}\|\widetilde{\varphi}^{(k)}_{j}\cdot P_{k}T_{m}^{i;\mu,\nu}(f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}},f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}})\|_{B^{1}_{k,j}}&\lesssim 2^{(1+\alpha)k}2^{(1/2+\beta)m}+2^{(3/2-\beta+\alpha)k}2^{m}.\end{split}$ (8.23) Using also ( 5.67 ), this gives the desired bound ( 8.3 ) if $\sigma_{1}\neq\sigma_{2}$ and ( 8.22 ) does not hold, since in this case the right-hand side of ( 8.23 ) is dominated by $C2^{-m/10}$ . If $\sigma_{1}=\sigma_{2}$ , and $k\leq-3m/4$ , then ( 8.3 ) follows from ( 8.23 ) and ( 5.67 ). If $k\geq-3m/4$ , since $\Lambda_{\sigma_{1}}$ is smooth when $\sigma_{1}\in\{e,b\}$ , we observe that $|\partial_{\eta}^{\rho}\Phi^{i;\sigma_{1}+,\sigma_{1}-}(\xi,\eta)|\lesssim_{\rho}2^{k},\quad\forall\,\,|\rho|\geq 2,\,\,\sigma_{1}\in\{e,b\},$ as long as $|\xi|\leq 2^{k+4}$ . Besides, from ( 8.2 ), we have that $\max(j_{1},j_{2})\leq(1-\beta/10)m+k$ . Therefore we can use Lemma A.2 with $K=2^{(1-\beta/20)m+k}$ , $\epsilon=2^{-\max(j_{1},j_{2})}$ to conclude that $|P_{k}T_{m}^{i;\mu,\nu}(f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}},f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}})(\xi)|\lesssim 2^{-10m}$ from which the desired inequality ( 8.3 ) follows easily.
∎ We consider now the remaining two phases. A key observation is the weak ellipticity bound $\lambda_{i}(a)+\lambda_{i}(b)-\lambda_{i}(a+b)\gtrsim_{C_{b},\varepsilon}a\min(1,b)^{2}\qquad\text{ if }0\leq a\leq b\text{ and }a\in[0,2^{-D/20}].$ (8.24) Indeed, using Lemma A.4 , if $b\leq r_{\ast}/2$ then $\lambda_{i}(a)+\lambda_{i}(b)-\lambda_{i}(a+b)=\int_{0}^{a}[\lambda^{\prime}(r)-\lambda^{\prime}(b+r)]\,dr=\int_{0}^{a}\int_{0}^{b}-\lambda^{\prime\prime}_{i}(r+s)\,drds\approx_{C_{b},\varepsilon}ab^{2}.$ On the other hand, if $b\geq r_{\ast}/2$ then $\lambda_{i}(a)+\lambda_{i}(b)-\lambda_{i}(a+b)=\int_{0}^{a}[\lambda^{\prime}(r)-\lambda^{\prime}(b+r)]\,dr\geq\int_{0}^{a}[q_{i}(r)+rq^{\prime}_{i}(r)-q_{i}(b+r)]\,dr\approx_{C_{b},\varepsilon}a,$ and the desired lower bound ( 8.24 ) follows. We prove first the required $L^{\infty}$ bounds. Lemma 8.5 . Assume that ( 8.2 ) holds and $\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}\in\in\{\Phi^{i;i+,i+},\Phi^{i;i+,i-}\}.$ Then, for any $\kappa\in(0,1]$ , $\kappa\geq\max\big(2^{(\beta^{2}m-m)/2}2^{-\min(k_{1},k_{2},0)/2}2^{-D/2},2^{\beta^{2}m-m}2^{\max(j_{1},j_{2})}\big)$ , we have $2^{(3/2+\alpha-\beta)k}\|\mathcal{F}P_{k}R_{m,\kappa}^{i;\mu,\nu}(f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}},f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}})\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{-2\beta^{4}m}.$ (8.25) Proof of Lemma 8.5 . Integration by parts in $s$ gives $\begin{split}\mathcal{F}T_{m}^{i;\mu,\nu}(f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}},f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}})(\xi)&=i\left[T_{1}(\xi)+T_{2}(\xi)+T_{3}(\xi)\right]\\
T_{1}(\xi)&:=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{e^{is\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}}{\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}q^{\prime}_{m}(s)\widehat{f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}}(\xi-\eta,s)\widehat{f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}}(\eta,s)d\eta ds\\
T_{2}(\xi)&:=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{e^{is\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}}{\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}q_{m}(s)(\partial_{s}\widehat{f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}})(\xi-\eta,s)\widehat{f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}}(\eta,s)d\eta ds\\
T_{3}(\xi)&:=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{e^{is\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}}{\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)}q_{m}(s)\widehat{f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}}(\xi-\eta,s)(\partial_{s}\widehat{f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}})(\eta,s)d\eta ds.\end{split}$ First, using ( 8.24 ), ( 5.17 ), and ( 5.20 ), we see that $\begin{split}2^{(3/2+\alpha-\beta)k}\|\varphi_{k}\cdot T_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}&\lesssim 2^{(3/2+\alpha-\beta)k}2^{-k-\widetilde{k_{1}}-\widetilde{k_{2}}}2^{m}\sup_{s\in[2^{m-4},2^{m+4}]}\|(\partial_{s}\widehat{f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}})(s)\|_{L^{2}}\|\widehat{f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\\
&\lesssim 2^{(3/2+\alpha-\beta)k}2^{-k-\widetilde{k_{1}}-\widetilde{k_{2}}}2^{m}\cdot 2^{\widetilde{k_{1}}}2^{-(1+\beta)m}2^{(1+\beta-\alpha)\widetilde{k_{2}}}\\
&\lesssim 2^{-\beta^{3}m}.\end{split}$ Similarly, $2^{(3/2+\alpha-\beta)k}\|\varphi_{k}\cdot T_{3}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{-\beta^{3}m}.$ Assuming that $|k|+|k_{1}|+|k_{2}|+|j_{1}|+|j_{2}|\geq\beta^{2}m,$ and using Hölder’s inequality, we find that $\begin{split}2^{(3/2+\alpha-\beta)k}&\|\varphi_{k}\cdot T_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{(3/2+\alpha-\beta)k}2^{-k-\widetilde{k_{1}}-\widetilde{k_{2}}}\sup_{s\in[2^{m-4},2^{m+4}]}\|\widehat{f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\|\widehat{f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\\
&\lesssim 2^{(3/2+\alpha-\beta)k}2^{-k-\widetilde{k_{1}}-\widetilde{k_{2}}}2^{2\beta(\widetilde{k_{1}}+\widetilde{k_{2}})}(2^{\alpha k_{1}}+2^{10k_{1}})^{-1}(2^{\alpha k_{2}}+2^{10k_{2}})^{-1}2^{-(1-\beta)(j_{1}+j_{2})}\\
&\lesssim 2^{-\beta^{3}m}.\end{split}$ On the other hand, if $|k|+|k_{1}|+|k_{2}|+|j_{1}|+|j_{2}|\leq\beta^{2}m,$ then we can decompose $T_{1}=T_{1;1}+T_{1;2}$ as in the proof of Lemma 8.2 and estimate also $2^{(3/2+\alpha-\beta)k}\|\varphi_{k}\cdot T_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{-\beta^{3}m}$ . The desired bound ( 8.25 ) follows using also ( 5.67 ).
∎ We prove now the weighted $L^{2}$ bounds, in two steps. Lemma 8.6 . Assume that ( 8.2 ) holds and $\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}\in\in\{\Phi^{i;i+,i+},\Phi^{i;i+,i-}\}.$ Then the $L^{2}$ bound $2^{(1+\alpha)k}2^{(1+\beta)j}\|P_{k}R_{m,\kappa}^{i;\mu,\nu}(f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}},f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}})\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-\beta^{4}m}$ (8.26) holds for any $\kappa\in(0,1]$ , $\kappa\geq\max\big(2^{(\beta^{2}m-m)/2}2^{-\min(k_{1},k_{2},0)/2}2^{-D/2},2^{\beta^{2}m-m}2^{\max(j_{1},j_{2})}\big)$ , provided that either $\max(k_{1},k_{2})\geq-D/10,$ (8.27) or $\max(k_{1},k_{2})\leq-D/10\qquad\text{ and }\qquad k+\min(k_{1},k_{2})/2\leq(\beta^{2}m-m)/2+D,$ (8.28) or $\max(k_{1},k_{2})\leq-D/10\qquad\text{ and }\qquad k+\max(k_{1},k_{2})+(1-\beta)j\leq 2D,$ (8.29) or $\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}=\Phi^{i;i+,i-}\qquad\text{ and }\qquad k_{2}\geq k_{1}+9,$ (8.30) or $\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}=\Phi^{i;i-,i+}\qquad\text{ and }\qquad k_{1}\geq k_{2}+9.$ (8.31) Proof of Lemma 8.6 . We use Lemma 8.2 first: the conclusion follows if either ( 8.30 ) or ( 8.31 ) are satisfied, since the lower bound ( 8.7 ) holds in these cases. The lower bound ( 8.7 ) also holds if $\max(k_{1},k_{2})\geq-D/10$ and $\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}=\Phi^{i;i+,i+}$ . On the other hand, if $\max(k_{1},k_{2})\geq-D/10$ and $\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}\in\in\{\Phi^{i;i+,i-}\}$ then $\begin{split}|(D^{\rho}_{\xi}\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu})(\xi,\eta)|&\lesssim 2^{k(1-|\rho|)},\\
|(D^{\rho}_{\eta}\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu})(\xi,\eta)|=|(D^{\rho}\Lambda_{i})(\eta-\xi)-(D^{\rho}\Lambda_{i})(\eta)|&\lesssim 2^{k},\\
|(D^{\rho_{1}}_{\xi}D^{\rho_{2}}_{\eta}\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu})(\xi,\eta)|&\lesssim 1,\\
|\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)|&\gtrsim 2^{k},\end{split}$ as long as $|\xi|\in[2^{k-6},2^{k+6}]$ , $|\xi-\eta|\in[2^{k_{1}-6},2^{k_{1}+6}]$ , $|\eta|\in[2^{k_{2}-6},2^{k_{2}+6}]$ , and $|\rho|\in[0,4]$ , $|\rho_{1}|,|\rho_{2}|\in[1,4]$ . The last bound follows from ( 8.24 ). We can use the formula ( 8.16 ) and integrate by parts as in the proof of Lemma 8.2 (ii) to conclude that ( 8.4 ) holds. The desired bound ( 8.26 ) follows from Lemma 8.2 (i). Assume now that ( 8.29 ) holds. We then estimate, using Plancherel, ( 5.18 ) and ( A.49 ), $\begin{split}\|T_{m}^{i;\mu,\nu}&(f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}},f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}})\|_{L^{2}}\\
&\lesssim 2^{m}\sup_{s\in[2^{m-4},2^{m+4}]}\min(\|Ef^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}\|f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}(s)\|_{L^{2}},\|f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\|Ef^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}})\\
&\lesssim 2^{m}\cdot 2^{-3/2m}2^{(2\beta-\alpha)(k_{1}+k_{2})}\min\big(2^{k_{1}/2}2^{(1/2+\beta)j_{1}}2^{-(1-\beta)j_{2}},2^{k_{2}/2}2^{(1/2+\beta)j_{2}}2^{-(1-\beta)j_{1}}\big)\\
&\lesssim 2^{-m/2}2^{(1/2+2\beta-\alpha)(k_{1}+k_{2})}(2^{-2\beta k_{1}}+2^{-2\beta k_{2}}).\end{split}$ Therefore, assuming for example that $k_{1}\geq k_{2}$ , $\begin{split}2^{(1+\alpha)k}2^{(1+\beta)j}\|T_{m}^{i;\mu,\nu}(f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}},f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}})\|_{L^{2}}&\lesssim 2^{(1+\alpha)k}2^{-(1+3\beta)(k+k_{1})}2^{-m/2}2^{(1+2\beta-2\alpha)k_{1}}\\
&\lesssim 2^{-3\beta(k+k_{1})}2^{-m/2}\\
&\lesssim 2^{-\beta^{2}m}\end{split}$ by ( 8.29 ). The desired bound ( 8.26 ) follows using also ( 5.67 ). Finally, assume that ( 8.28 ) holds. Assume first that $k\leq\min(k,k_{1},k_{2})+10$ and $\max(j_{1},j_{2})\geq 3\beta m$ or $\max(k_{1},k_{2})\leq-3\beta m/2$ . In this case, we may simply use Plancherel, ( 5.18 ) and ( A.49 ) to estimate $\begin{split}\|T_{m}^{i;\mu,\nu}&(f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}},f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}})\|_{L^{2}}\\
&\lesssim 2^{m}\sup_{s\in[2^{m-4},2^{m+4}]}\min(\|Ef^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}\|f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}(s)\|_{L^{2}},\|f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\|Ef^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}})\\
&\lesssim 2^{m}\cdot 2^{-3/2m}2^{(1/2+3\beta)\max(k_{1},k_{2})}2^{(1/2+\beta)(\min(j_{1},j_{2})-\max(j_{1},j_{2}))}2^{-(1/2-2\beta)\max(j_{1},j_{2})},\end{split}$ and therefore, assuming for example that $k_{1}\geq k_{2}$ , $\begin{split}2^{(1+\alpha)k}2^{(1+\beta)j}\|T_{m}^{i;\mu,\nu}(f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}},f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}})\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{(1+\alpha)[k+k_{1}/2]}2^{(1/2+\beta)m}2^{-(1/2-2\beta)\max(j_{1},j_{2})}2^{(11\beta/4)k_{1}}\lesssim 2^{-\beta^{3}m}.\end{split}$ The desired bound ( 8.26 ) follows using also ( 5.67 ). Assume now that $\min(k_{1},k_{2})\leq k-10$ . In this case $k\geq\max(k_{1},k_{2})-4$ and necessarily $\max(j_{1},j_{2})\geq m/8$ by ( 8.28 ). We may assume that $k_{2}\leq k_{1}$ . If $j_{1}\leq j_{2}-6\beta m$ , then, using Plancherel, ( 5.18 ) and ( A.49 ), we get that $\begin{split}\|T_{m}^{i;\mu,\nu}(f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}},f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}})\|_{L^{2}}&\lesssim 2^{m}\sup_{s\in[2^{m-4},2^{m+4}]}\|Ef^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}\|f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\\
&\lesssim 2^{m}\cdot 2^{-3/2m}2^{k_{1}/2}2^{(1/2+\beta)j_{1}}2^{-(1-\beta)j_{2}}2^{3/2\beta(k_{1}+k_{2})}\\
\end{split}$ and therefore, $\begin{split}2^{(1+\alpha)k}2^{(1+\beta)j}\|T_{m}^{i;\mu,\nu}(f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}},f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}})\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{(1+\alpha)[k+k_{2}/2]}2^{(1/2+\beta)m}2^{-(1/2+\beta/4)(j_{1}-j_{2})}2^{3/4\beta j_{1}}\lesssim 2^{-\beta^{3}m}.\end{split}$ The desired bound ( 8.26 ) follows using also ( 5.67 ).
If $j_{1}\geq j_{2}-6\beta m$ and $\max(j_{1},j_{2})\geq m/8$ , then $(1/2+\beta)j_{2}-(1-\beta)j_{1}\leq-(1/2-2\beta)j_{1}+2\beta m+4\beta^{2}m\leq-4\beta m$ and using Plancherel, ( 5.18 ) and ( A.49 ), we get that $\begin{split}\|T_{m}^{i;\mu,\nu}(f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}},f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}})\|_{L^{2}}&\lesssim 2^{m}\sup_{s\in[2^{m-4},2^{m+4}]}\|f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\|Ef^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}\\
&\lesssim 2^{m}\cdot 2^{-3/2m}2^{k_{2}/2}2^{(1/2+\beta)j_{2}}2^{-(1-\beta)j_{1}}2^{3/2\beta(k_{1}+k_{2})}\\
\end{split}$ and therefore, $\begin{split}2^{(1+\alpha)k}2^{(1+\beta)j}\|T_{m}^{i;\mu,\nu}(f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}},f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}})\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{(1+\alpha)[k+k_{2}/2]}2^{(1/2+\beta)m}2^{[(1/2+\beta)j_{2}-(1-\beta)j_{1})}2^{3/2\beta(k_{1}+k_{2}/2)}\lesssim 2^{-\beta^{3}m}.\end{split}$ The desired bound ( 8.26 ) follows using also ( 5.67 ). Finally, assume that $k\leq(2\beta m-m)/2+D,\qquad\max(j_{1},j_{2})\leq 3\beta m,\qquad-D/10\geq k_{1}\geq k_{2}\geq-3\beta m/2-10.$ If $\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}=\Phi^{i;i+,i+}$ then the lower bound ( 8.7 ) holds, and the desired conclusion follows from Lemma 8.2 . On the other hand, if $\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}=\Phi^{i;i+,i-}$ , then we claim that ( 8.5 ) holds. Indeed, using also ( 8.24 ), $\begin{split}|\Phi^{i;i+,i-}(\xi,\eta)|&\gtrsim 2^{k+2k_{1}},\\
|D^{\rho}_{\xi}\Phi^{i;i+,i-}(\xi,\eta)|&\lesssim 2^{(1-|\rho|)k},\,\,\quad|\rho|\geq 1,\\
|D^{\rho}_{\eta}\Phi^{i;i+,i-}(\xi,\eta)|&\lesssim 2^{k}2^{-|\rho|k_{1}},\quad|\rho|\geq 1\\
|D_{\xi}^{\rho_{1}}D^{\rho_{2}}_{\eta}\Phi^{i;i+,i-}(\xi,\eta)|&\lesssim 2^{(1-|\rho_{1}|-|\rho_{2}|)k_{1}},\quad|\rho_{1}|\cdot|\rho_{2}|\geq 1.\end{split}$ We use ( 8.16 ) and proceed as in the proof of Lemma 8.2 (ii). The desired bound ( 8.5 ) follows and we can apply Lemma 8.2 to complete the proof.
∎ In view of Lemma 8.4 and Lemma 8.6 , for Proposition 8.1 it remains to prove the following lemma. Lemma 8.7 . Assume that $\Phi^{\sigma;\mu,\nu}\in\{\Phi^{i;i+,i+},\Phi^{i;i+,i-}\}$ and that $\begin{split}&-9m/10\leq k_{1},k_{2}\leq j/N_{0}^{\prime},\quad\max(j_{1},j_{2})\leq(1-\beta/10)m+k,\quad k_{2}\leq k_{1}+9\leq-D/10+9,\\
&\beta m/2+D^{2}\leq j\leq m+D,\quad(1-\beta)j\geq-k-k_{1}+D,\\
&(\beta^{2}m-m)+2D\leq 2k+k_{2}\leq-D/2,\end{split}$ (8.32) and set $\kappa:=2^{k-D}$ . Then $2^{(1+\alpha)k}2^{(1+\beta)j}\|\widetilde{\varphi}^{(k)}_{j}\cdot P_{k}R^{i;\mu,\nu}_{m,\kappa}(f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}},f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}})\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-2\beta^{4}m}.$ (8.33) Note that, in view of ( 8.32 ), $\kappa=2^{k-D}$ satisfies the necessary assumption $\kappa\geq\max\big(2^{(\beta^{2}m-m)/2}2^{-\min(k_{1},k_{2},0)/2}2^{-D/2},2^{\beta^{2}m-m}2^{\max(j_{1},j_{2})}\big).$ We remark that Lemma 8.7 covers the case of $k_{2}\leq k_{1}$ for $\Phi^{i;i+,i+}$ . By symmetry, this also covers the case $k_{1}\leq k_{2}$ . Proof of Lemma 8.7 . In this lemma we need to use finer decompositions and additional orthogonality arguments. We will use repeatedly the following observation from the sine law in the triangle formed by $\xi$ , $\xi-\eta$ , $\eta$ : $\frac{\sin(\angle(\xi,\eta))}{|\xi-\eta|}=\frac{\sin(\angle(\xi,\xi-\eta))}{|\eta|}=\frac{\sin(\angle(\xi-\eta,\eta))}{|\xi|}.$ (8.34) We need to introduce some angular localizations. For $\delta<1/10$ to be fixed in each case below, we choose $\{\omega_{i}\}_{i}$ a maximal family of $\delta$ -separated points on the sphere which is symmetrical in the sense that $\omega\in\{\omega_{i}\}\Rightarrow-\omega\in\{\omega_{i}\}$ ,
and we define $\begin{split}\chi_{e_{1}}^{0}(\xi)&=\varphi(2^{-k}\xi_{1}-1)\varphi(2^{-k}\delta^{-1}\xi_{2})\varphi(2^{-k}\delta^{-1}\xi_{3}),\quad\chi_{\omega_{i}}^{0}(\xi)=\chi_{e_{1}}^{0}(R_{\omega_{i}}\xi)\end{split}$ (8.35) where $R_{\omega_{i}}$ is a rotation satisfying $R_{\omega_{i}}(\omega_{i})=e_{1}$ .
We similarly define $\begin{split}\chi_{e_{1}}^{1}(\eta)&=\varphi(2^{-k_{1}-10}\eta_{1}-1)\varphi(2^{-k_{1}-10}\delta^{-1}\eta_{2})\varphi(2^{-k_{1}-10}\delta^{-1}\eta_{3}),\quad\chi^{1}_{\omega_{i}}(\eta)=\chi^{1}_{e_{1}}(R_{\omega_{i}}\eta)\\
\chi_{e_{1}}^{2}(\eta)&=\varphi(2^{-k_{2}-10}\eta_{1}-1)\varphi(2^{-k_{2}-10}\delta^{-1}\eta_{2})\varphi(2^{-k_{2}-10}\delta^{-1}\eta_{3}),\quad\chi^{2}_{\omega_{i}}(\eta)=\chi^{2}_{e_{1}}(R_{\omega_{i}}\eta).\end{split}$ (8.36) We then define the corresponding operators $Q^{j}_{\omega}$ by the formulas $\widehat{Q^{j}_{\omega}f}(\xi)=\chi^{j}_{\omega}(\xi)\widehat{f}(\xi).$ Since $\{\omega_{i}\}$ is maximal, there holds that, for any $f\in L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})$ , $\|f\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\sum_{\omega_{i}}\|Q^{j}_{\omega_{i}}f\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\|f\|_{L^{2}}^{2},\quad 0\leq j\leq 2,$ (8.37) uniformly in $k$ , $k_{1}$ , $k_{2}$ , $\delta$ . In addition, $Q^{j}_{\omega}$ satisfies $\|Q^{j}_{\omega}f\|_{L^{p}}\lesssim\|f\|_{L^{p}},\quad 0\leq j\leq 2,\,\,1\leq p\leq\infty.$ (8.38) Case 1 : assume that $j\geq m+k_{2}+D.$ In this case, we only need a coarse localization which separates the poles and we use the finite speed of propagation to treat parallel interactions. We set $\delta=2^{-D/10}$ in the definition ( 8.35 )-( 8.36 ) and decompose $R^{i;\mu,\nu}_{m,\kappa}(f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}},f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}})=\sum_{\omega_{0},\omega_{1},\omega_{2}}Q^{0}_{\omega_{0}}R^{i;\mu,\nu}_{m,\kappa}(Q^{1}_{\omega_{1}}f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}},Q^{2}_{\omega_{2}}f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}}).$ (8.39) Since this sum is finite, we need only show that for any choice of $\omega_{0},\omega_{1},\omega_{2}$ , there holds that $2^{(1+\alpha)k}2^{(1+\beta)j}\|\varphi^{(k)}_{j}\cdot P_{k}Q^{0}_{\omega_{0}}R^{i;\mu,\nu}_{m,\kappa}(Q^{1}_{\omega_{1}}f^{\mu}_{k_{1},j_{1}},Q^{2}_{\omega_{2}}f^{\nu}_{k_{2},j_{2}})\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-\beta^{3}m}$ (8.40) Indeed, we need only consider two subcases. $(i)$ If $\angle(\omega_{0},\omega_{1})>2^{-D/20}$ , then, in case $\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}=\Phi^{i;i+,i+}$ , we find that $|\Xi^{i+,i+}(\xi,\eta)|\gtrsim\angle(\omega_{1},\omega_{2})$ (8.41) and therefore ( 8.40 ) follows trivially. In case $\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}=\Phi^{i;i+,i-}$ , we claim that $\begin{split}&\Big\|\mathcal{F}\Big\{\frac{\chi^{0}_{\omega_{0}}(\xi)\chi^{1}_{\omega_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\chi^{2}_{\omega_{2}}(\eta)}{\Phi^{i;i+,i-}(\xi,\eta)}\varphi_{[k-4,k+4]}(\xi)\varphi_{[k_{1}-4,k_{1}+4]}(\xi-\eta)\varphi_{[k_{2}-4,k_{2}+4]}(\eta)\Big\}\Big\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}\lesssim 2^{-k}.\end{split}$ (8.42) Assuming ( 8.42 ), the estimate ( 8.40 ) follows from Lemma 8.3 . To prove ( 8.42 ), we first observe that, using ( 8.24 ) and Lemma A.4 , $\begin{split}\Phi^{i;i+,i-}(\xi,\eta)&=\lambda_{i}(|\xi|)+\lambda_{i}(|\eta|)-\lambda_{i}(|\xi|+|\eta|)+\left[\lambda_{i}(|\xi|+|\eta|)-\lambda_{i}(|\xi-\eta|)\right]\\
&\geq\lambda_{i}(|\xi|+|\eta|)-\lambda_{i}(|\xi-\eta|)\\
&\gtrsim|\xi|+|\eta|-|\xi-\eta|\\
&\gtrsim|\xi|\left[1-\cos(\angle\xi,\xi-\eta)\right]+|\eta|\left[1-\cos(\angle\eta-\xi,\eta)\right]\\
&\gtrsim|\xi|\end{split}$ and the inequalities $\begin{split}|\Phi^{i;i+,i-}(\xi,\eta)|&\geq\lambda_{i}(|\xi|)\gtrsim 2^{k},\\
|D^{\rho}_{\xi}\Phi^{i;i+,i-}(\xi,\eta)|&\lesssim 2^{(1-|\rho|)k},\quad|\rho|\geq 1,\\
|D^{\rho}_{\eta}\Phi^{i;i+,i-}(\xi,\eta)|&\lesssim 2^{k}2^{-|\rho|k_{2}},\quad|\rho|\geq 1,\\
|D_{\xi}^{\rho_{1}}D^{\rho_{2}}_{\eta}\Phi^{i;i+,i-}(\xi,\eta)|&\lesssim 2^{(1-|\rho_{1}|-|\rho_{2}|)k_{1}},\quad|\rho_{1}|\cdot|\rho_{2}|\geq 1,\end{split}$ hold in the support of the multiplier in ( 8.42 ). Using the formula ( 8.16 ) and integrating by parts we derive the inequality ( 8.42 ). $(ii)$ if $\angle(\omega_{0},\omega_{1})\leq 2^{-D/20}$ , we claim that, on the support of $\begin{split}&\chi^{i;\mu,\nu}_{C}(\xi,\eta):=\\
&\varphi(2^{D^{2}}\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta))\varphi(2^{D-k}|\Xi^{\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)|)\varphi_{k}(\xi)\varphi_{[k_{1}-2,k_{1}+2]}(\xi-\eta)\varphi_{[k_{2}-2,k_{2}+2]}(\eta)\chi^{0}_{\omega_{0}}(\xi)\chi^{1}_{\omega_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\chi^{2}_{\omega_{2}}(\eta)\end{split}$ we have that $|\nabla_{\xi}\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)|\lesssim 2^{k_{2}}.$ (8.43) Indeed, on the support of $\chi^{i;\mu,\nu}_{C}$ , we have that $\angle(\xi,\xi-\eta)\lesssim\sin(\angle(\xi,\xi-\eta))$ and therefore, using also ( 8.34 ) and the smallness of $|\Xi^{\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)|$ , if $\chi^{i;\mu,\nu}_{C}(\xi,\eta)\neq 0$ , then $\begin{split}|\nabla_{\xi}\Phi^{i;\mu,\nu}(\xi,\eta)|&\lesssim|\lambda_{i}^{\prime}(|\xi|)-\lambda_{i}^{\prime}(|\xi-\eta|)|+\angle(\xi,\xi-\eta)\\
&\lesssim 2^{k_{1}+k_{2}}+2^{k_{2}-k}\sin(\angle(\xi-\eta,\eta))\\
